// This function simulates what your React frontend will do
function simulateFrontendRequests() {
  Logger.log('=== SIMULATING FRONTEND REQUESTS ===');

  try {
    // Simulate GET request for companies
    const getCompaniesResult = doGet({
      parameter: { action: 'getCompanies' }
    });
    Logger.log('GET companies result:', getCompaniesResult.getContent());

    // Simulate POST request to create company
    const createCompanyResult = doPost({
      postData: {
        contents: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'createCompany',
          payload: {
            name: 'Frontend Test Company',
            code: 'FRONT'
          }
        })
      }
    });
    Logger.log('POST create company result:', createCompanyResult.getContent());

    // Simulate ping request
    const pingResult = doGet({
      parameter: { action: 'ping' }
    });
    Logger.log('Ping result:', pingResult.getContent());

    Logger.log('=== FRONTEND SIMULATION COMPLETED ===');

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error simulating frontend requests:', error.message);
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * RUNTIME ERROR VERIFICATION SUITE
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Comprehensive runtime error verification for all Apps Script functions.
 * This function tests each function individually to ensure no runtime errors.
 */
function verifyAllFunctionsRuntimeSafety() {
  Logger.log('=== STARTING RUNTIME SAFETY VERIFICATION ===');

  const results = {
    totalFunctions: 0,
    passedFunctions: 0,
    failedFunctions: 0,
    errors: []
  };

  // Test Core API Functions
  try {
    Logger.log('Testing Core API Functions...');

    // Test doGet with valid parameters
    const pingTest = doGet({ parameter: { action: 'ping' } });
    if (pingTest) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    // Test doPost with valid parameters
    const postTest = doPost({
      postData: { contents: JSON.stringify({ action: 'ping' }) }
    });
    if (postTest) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    // Test doOptions
    const optionsTest = doOptions({});
    if (optionsTest) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    Logger.log('Core API Functions: ✅ PASSED');
  } catch (error) {
    results.errors.push(`Core API Functions: ${error.message}`);
    results.failedFunctions++;
  }

  // Test Utility Functions
  try {
    Logger.log('Testing Utility Functions...');

    // Test generateId
    const idTest = generateId('test');
    if (idTest && idTest.includes('test_')) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    // Test createJsonResponse
    const jsonTest = createJsonResponse({ test: 'data' });
    if (jsonTest) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    // Test pingAPI
    const pingApiTest = pingAPI();
    if (pingApiTest && pingApiTest.status) results.passedFunctions++; else results.failedFunctions++;
    results.totalFunctions++;

    Logger.log('Utility Functions: ✅ PASSED');
  } catch (error) {
    results.errors.push(`Utility Functions: ${error.message}`);
    results.failedFunctions++;
  }

  // Test CRUD Functions (with mock parameters)
  try {
    Logger.log('Testing CRUD Function Structure...');

    // Note: These tests check function structure without actually modifying data
    const crudFunctions = [
      'getCompanies', 'createCompany', 'updateCompany', 'deleteCompany',
      'getRoles', 'createRole', 'updateRole', 'deleteRole',
      'getDropdownLists', 'createDropdownList', 'updateDropdownList', 'deleteDropdownList',
      'getTicketTypes', 'createTicketType', 'updateTicketType', 'deleteTicketType'
    ];

    // Check if functions exist and are callable
    crudFunctions.forEach(funcName => {
      try {
        if (typeof eval(funcName) === 'function') {
          results.passedFunctions++;
          Logger.log(`${funcName}: ✅ Function exists`);
        } else {
          results.failedFunctions++;
          results.errors.push(`${funcName}: Function not found`);
        }
        results.totalFunctions++;
      } catch (error) {
        results.failedFunctions++;
        results.errors.push(`${funcName}: ${error.message}`);
        results.totalFunctions++;
      }
    });

    Logger.log('CRUD Functions Structure: ✅ PASSED');
  } catch (error) {
    results.errors.push(`CRUD Functions: ${error.message}`);
  }

  // Test Sheet Initialization Functions
  try {
    Logger.log('Testing Sheet Initialization Functions...');

    const initFunctions = [
      'initializeCompaniesSheet', 'initializeRolesSheet', 'initializeDropdownListsSheet',
      'initializeDropdownOptionsSheet', 'initializeTicketTypesSheet', 'initializeAdminActionLogsSheet'
    ];

    initFunctions.forEach(funcName => {
      try {
        if (typeof eval(funcName) === 'function') {
          results.passedFunctions++;
          Logger.log(`${funcName}: ✅ Function exists`);
        } else {
          results.failedFunctions++;
          results.errors.push(`${funcName}: Function not found`);
        }
        results.totalFunctions++;
      } catch (error) {
        results.failedFunctions++;
        results.errors.push(`${funcName}: ${error.message}`);
        results.totalFunctions++;
      }
    });

    Logger.log('Sheet Initialization Functions: ✅ PASSED');
  } catch (error) {
    results.errors.push(`Sheet Initialization Functions: ${error.message}`);
  }

  // Generate Final Report
  Logger.log('=== RUNTIME SAFETY VERIFICATION COMPLETE ===');
  Logger.log(`Total Functions Tested: ${results.totalFunctions}`);
  Logger.log(`Passed: ${results.passedFunctions}`);
  Logger.log(`Failed: ${results.failedFunctions}`);
  Logger.log(`Success Rate: ${Math.round((results.passedFunctions / results.totalFunctions) * 100)}%`);

  if (results.errors.length > 0) {
    Logger.log('=== ERRORS FOUND ===');
    results.errors.forEach(error => Logger.log(`❌ ${error}`));
  } else {
    Logger.log('🎉 ALL FUNCTIONS PASSED RUNTIME SAFETY VERIFICATION');
  }

  return results;
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * DIAGNOSTIC FUNCTIONS FOR SCHEMA DEBUGGING
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Diagnostic function to check dropdown_lists sheet structure and data mapping
 */
function diagnoseDropdownListsSheet() {
  try {
    Logger.log("=== DROPDOWN LISTS SHEET DIAGNOSIS ===");

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      Logger.log("Sheet does not exist, creating...");
      listsSheet = initializeDropdownListsSheet();
    }

    // Get all data including headers
    const allData = listsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    Logger.log(`Total rows: ${allData.length}`);

    if (allData.length === 0) {
      Logger.log("No data in sheet");
      return;
    }

    // Log headers
    const headers = allData[0];
    Logger.log(`Headers (${headers.length} columns): ${JSON.stringify(headers)}`);

    // Expected headers
    const expectedHeaders = ['id', 'name', 'description', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'];
    Logger.log(`Expected (${expectedHeaders.length} columns): ${JSON.stringify(expectedHeaders)}`);

    // Compare headers
    const headerMatch = JSON.stringify(headers) === JSON.stringify(expectedHeaders);
    Logger.log(`Headers match expected: ${headerMatch}`);

    if (!headerMatch) {
      Logger.log("Header differences:");
      for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(headers.length, expectedHeaders.length); i++) {
        if (headers[i] !== expectedHeaders[i]) {
          Logger.log(`  Position ${i}: Got '${headers[i]}', Expected '${expectedHeaders[i]}'`);
        }
      }
    }

    // Log sample data
    if (allData.length > 1) {
      Logger.log(`Sample data rows: ${allData.length - 1}`);
      for (let i = 1; i <= Math.min(3, allData.length - 1); i++) {
        const row = allData[i];
        Logger.log(`Row ${i}: ${JSON.stringify(row)}`);

        // Map row to object manually
        const obj = {};
        headers.forEach((header, index) => {
          obj[header] = row[index];
        });
        Logger.log(`Row ${i} as object: ${JSON.stringify(obj)}`);
      }
    }

    // Test getSheetDataAsJSON function
    Logger.log("Testing getSheetDataAsJSON function:");
    const jsonData = getSheetDataAsJSON(listsSheet);
    Logger.log(`getSheetDataAsJSON returned ${jsonData.length} objects`);
    if (jsonData.length > 0) {
      Logger.log(`First object: ${JSON.stringify(jsonData[0])}`);
      Logger.log(`Object keys: ${Object.keys(jsonData[0])}`);
    }

    return {
      totalRows: allData.length,
      headers: headers,
      expectedHeaders: expectedHeaders,
      headerMatch: headerMatch,
      sampleData: jsonData.slice(0, 3)
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in diagnoseDropdownListsSheet: ${error.message}`);
    Logger.log(`Stack: ${error.stack}`);
    return { error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Migration function to convert old 4-column dropdown data to new 12-column format
 */
function migrateDropdownListsTo12Columns() {
  try {
    Logger.log("=== MIGRATING DROPDOWN LISTS TO 12 COLUMNS ===");

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      Logger.log("No dropdown_lists sheet found - nothing to migrate");
      return { success: true, message: "No sheet to migrate" };
    }

    // Get all data
    const allData = listsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (allData.length <= 1) {
      Logger.log("No data rows to migrate");
      return { success: true, message: "No data to migrate" };
    }

    const headers = allData[0];
    Logger.log(`Current headers: ${JSON.stringify(headers)}`);

    // Check if migration is needed
    const expectedHeaders = ['id', 'name', 'description', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason'];

    if (headers.length === 12 && JSON.stringify(headers) === JSON.stringify(expectedHeaders)) {
      Logger.log("Headers are already correct - checking data format");

      // Check if data needs migration (look for old 4-column format)
      let needsMigration = false;
      for (let i = 1; i < allData.length; i++) {
        const row = allData[i];
        // Old format: data in positions 0,1,2,3 and empty in positions 4-11
        // New format: data properly distributed across all 12 columns
        if (row.length >= 12 && row[2] && row[3] && !row[4] && !row[5]) {
          // Check if positions 2,3 contain timestamps (old format)
          const col2 = String(row[2]);
          const col3 = String(row[3]);
          if (col2.includes('T') && col2.includes('Z') && col3.includes('T') && col3.includes('Z')) {
            needsMigration = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if (!needsMigration) {
        Logger.log("Data format is already correct - no migration needed");
        return { success: true, message: "Data already in correct format" };
      }
    }

    Logger.log(`Migrating ${allData.length - 1} data rows...`);

    // Clear the sheet and rewrite with correct headers
    listsSheet.clear();
    listsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 12).setValues([expectedHeaders]);
    listsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 12).setFontWeight('bold');

    let migratedCount = 0;
    const currentTime = new Date().toISOString();
    const currentUser = "system_migration";

    // Migrate each data row
    for (let i = 1; i < allData.length; i++) {
      const oldRow = allData[i];

      if (oldRow.length >= 4 && oldRow[0] && oldRow[1]) {
        // Old format: [id, name, created_at, updated_at, ...]
        // New format: [id, name, description, company_id, is_active, created_at, created_by, updated_at, updated_by, deactivated_at, deactivated_by, deactivation_reason]

        const newRow = [
          oldRow[0],                    // id (keep same)
          oldRow[1],                    // name (keep same)
          "",                           // description (new field - empty)
          null,                         // company_id (new field - null for global)
          true,                         // is_active (new field - default true)
          oldRow[2] || currentTime,     // created_at (from old position 2)
          currentUser,                  // created_by (new field - system)
          oldRow[3] || currentTime,     // updated_at (from old position 3)
          currentUser,                  // updated_by (new field - system)
          null,                         // deactivated_at (new field - null)
          null,                         // deactivated_by (new field - null)
          null                          // deactivation_reason (new field - null)
        ];

        listsSheet.appendRow(newRow);
        migratedCount++;
        Logger.log(`Migrated row ${i}: ${oldRow[0]} - ${oldRow[1]}`);
      }
    }

    Logger.log(`=== MIGRATION COMPLETE ===`);
    Logger.log(`Successfully migrated ${migratedCount} dropdown lists`);

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `Migrated ${migratedCount} rows`,
      migratedCount: migratedCount
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in migrateDropdownListsTo12Columns: ${error.message}`);
    Logger.log(`Stack: ${error.stack}`);
    return { success: false, error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * CLEAN RESET: Clear all data and reinitialize with correct schemas
 * This is faster than migration for proof of concept
 */
function cleanResetAllTables() {
  try {
    Logger.log("=== CLEAN RESET: CLEARING ALL TABLES ===");

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const results = {
      success: true,
      tablesReset: [],
      errors: []
    };

    // Tables that need clean reset (only include tables with existing init functions)
    const tablesToReset = [
      { name: 'dropdown_lists', initFunction: 'initializeDropdownListsSheet' },
      { name: 'dropdown_options', initFunction: 'initializeDropdownOptionsSheet' },
      { name: 'companies', initFunction: 'initializeCompaniesSheet' },
      { name: 'roles', initFunction: 'initializeRolesSheet' },
      { name: 'ticket_types', initFunction: 'initializeTicketTypesSheet' },
      { name: 'admin_action_logs', initFunction: 'initializeAdminActionLogsSheet' }
      // Note: custom_fields and workflow_steps init functions not yet implemented
    ];

    tablesToReset.forEach(table => {
      try {
        Logger.log(`Resetting ${table.name}...`);

        const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(table.name);
        if (sheet) {
          // Delete the entire sheet
          ss.deleteSheet(sheet);
          Logger.log(`  Deleted existing ${table.name} sheet`);
        }

        // Recreate with correct schema
        if (typeof eval(table.initFunction) === 'function') {
          eval(table.initFunction + '()');
          Logger.log(`  Recreated ${table.name} with correct schema`);
          results.tablesReset.push(table.name);
        } else {
          Logger.log(`  Warning: ${table.initFunction} not found`);
          results.errors.push(`${table.name}: Init function not found`);
        }

      } catch (error) {
        Logger.log(`  Error resetting ${table.name}: ${error.message}`);
        results.errors.push(`${table.name}: ${error.message}`);
      }
    });

    Logger.log(`=== CLEAN RESET COMPLETE ===`);
    Logger.log(`Tables reset: ${results.tablesReset.join(', ')}`);

    if (results.errors.length > 0) {
      Logger.log(`Errors: ${results.errors.join(', ')}`);
      results.success = false;
    }

    return results;

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in cleanResetAllTables: ${error.message}`);
    return { success: false, error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Create fresh test data after clean reset
 */
function createFreshTestData() {
  try {
    Logger.log("=== CREATING FRESH TEST DATA ===");

    const results = {
      success: true,
      created: [],
      errors: []
    };

    // Create test companies
    try {
      const company1 = createCompany({
        name: "Test Company A",
        code: "TCA"
      });
      const company2 = createCompany({
        name: "Test Company B",
        code: "TCB"
      });
      results.created.push(`Companies: ${company1.name}, ${company2.name}`);
      Logger.log("✅ Created test companies");
    } catch (error) {
      results.errors.push(`Companies: ${error.message}`);
    }

    // Create test roles
    try {
      const globalRole = createRole({
        name: "Admin",
        company_id: null  // Global role
      });
      results.created.push(`Roles: ${globalRole.name} (Global)`);
      Logger.log("✅ Created test roles");
    } catch (error) {
      results.errors.push(`Roles: ${error.message}`);
    }

    // Create test dropdown lists
    try {
      const dropdown1 = createDropdownList({
        name: "Priority Levels",
        description: "Task priority options",
        company_id: null,  // Global dropdown
        options: [
          { label: "High", value: "high" },
          { label: "Medium", value: "medium" },
          { label: "Low", value: "low" }
        ]
      });

      const dropdown2 = createDropdownList({
        name: "Status Types",
        description: "Request status options",
        company_id: null,  // Global dropdown
        options: [
          { label: "Open", value: "open" },
          { label: "In Progress", value: "progress" },
          { label: "Closed", value: "closed" }
        ]
      });

      results.created.push(`Dropdowns: ${dropdown1.name}, ${dropdown2.name}`);
      Logger.log("✅ Created test dropdowns with options");
    } catch (error) {
      results.errors.push(`Dropdowns: ${error.message}`);
    }

    Logger.log(`=== FRESH TEST DATA COMPLETE ===`);
    Logger.log(`Created: ${results.created.join(', ')}`);

    if (results.errors.length > 0) {
      Logger.log(`Errors: ${results.errors.join(', ')}`);
      results.success = false;
    }

    return results;

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in createFreshTestData: ${error.message}`);
    return { success: false, error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Complete reset and test data creation (one-click solution)
 */
function completeSystemReset() {
  Logger.log("=== COMPLETE SYSTEM RESET ===");

  const resetResult = cleanResetAllTables();
  if (!resetResult.success) {
    Logger.log("Reset failed, aborting test data creation");
    return resetResult;
  }

  const testDataResult = createFreshTestData();

  return {
    success: resetResult.success && testDataResult.success,
    reset: resetResult,
    testData: testDataResult,
    message: "System reset complete - ready for testing"
  };
}

/**
 * Diagnostic function to test immediate dropdown creation and retrieval
 * Tests if newly created dropdowns are immediately available via filtering
 */
function testDropdownCreationAndRetrieval(payload) {
  Logger.log("=== DROPDOWN CREATION AND RETRIEVAL TEST ===");
  Logger.log(`Input payload: ${JSON.stringify(payload)}`);

  try {
    // Step 1: Create the dropdown
    Logger.log("Step 1: Creating dropdown...");
    const createdDropdown = createDropdownList(payload);
    Logger.log(`Created dropdown: ${JSON.stringify(createdDropdown)}`);

    // Step 2: Immediately fetch all dropdowns (no filter)
    Logger.log("Step 2: Fetching all dropdowns...");
    const allDropdowns = getDropdownLists();
    Logger.log(`Total dropdowns found: ${allDropdowns.length}`);

    const foundInAll = allDropdowns.find(d => d.id === createdDropdown.id);
    Logger.log(`Created dropdown found in all: ${foundInAll ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`);
    if (foundInAll) {
      Logger.log(`Found data: ${JSON.stringify(foundInAll)}`);
    }

    // Step 3: Fetch dropdowns filtered by company
    if (createdDropdown.company_id) {
      Logger.log(`Step 3: Fetching dropdowns filtered by company_id: ${createdDropdown.company_id}`);
      const filteredDropdowns = getDropdownLists({company_id: createdDropdown.company_id});
      Logger.log(`Filtered dropdowns found: ${filteredDropdowns.length}`);

      const foundInFiltered = filteredDropdowns.find(d => d.id === createdDropdown.id);
      Logger.log(`Created dropdown found in filtered: ${foundInFiltered ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`);
      if (foundInFiltered) {
        Logger.log(`Filtered data: ${JSON.stringify(foundInFiltered)}`);
      }

      // Step 4: Debug filtering logic
      Logger.log("Step 4: Debugging filtering logic...");
      Logger.log(`Created dropdown company_id type: ${typeof createdDropdown.company_id}`);
      Logger.log(`Created dropdown company_id value: "${createdDropdown.company_id}"`);

      // Check each dropdown in all results
      allDropdowns.forEach((dropdown, index) => {
        if (dropdown.id === createdDropdown.id) {
          Logger.log(`Found our dropdown at index ${index}:`);
          Logger.log(`  ID: ${dropdown.id}`);
          Logger.log(`  Name: ${dropdown.name}`);
          Logger.log(`  Company ID: "${dropdown.company_id}" (type: ${typeof dropdown.company_id})`);
          Logger.log(`  Match test: ${dropdown.company_id === createdDropdown.company_id}`);
        }
      });
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      created: createdDropdown,
      totalDropdowns: allDropdowns.length,
      foundInAll: !!foundInAll,
      foundInFiltered: createdDropdown.company_id ? !!filteredDropdowns.find(d => d.id === createdDropdown.id) : null
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in testDropdownCreationAndRetrieval: ${error.message}`);
    Logger.log(`Error stack: ${error.stack}`);
    return {
      success: false,
      error: error.message
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Comprehensive end-to-end verification of dropdown creation flow
 * Tests the complete pipeline: doPost → createDropdownList → sheet write → sheet read
 */
function verifyDropdownSavingPipeline() {
  Logger.log("=== COMPLETE DROPDOWN SAVING PIPELINE VERIFICATION ===");

  try {
    // Step 1: Test direct sheet write (bypass API layer)
    Logger.log("Step 1: Testing direct sheet write...");
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      listsSheet = initializeDropdownListsSheet();
    }

    const testId = generateId('test');
    const testData = [
      testId, 'Direct Test List', 'Direct write test', 'comp_test_123', true,
      new Date().toISOString(), 'test_user', new Date().toISOString(), 'test_user',
      null, null, null
    ];

    const beforeRowCount = listsSheet.getLastRow();
    listsSheet.appendRow(testData);
    const afterRowCount = listsSheet.getLastRow();

    Logger.log(`Direct write - Before: ${beforeRowCount}, After: ${afterRowCount}`);
    const directWriteSuccess = afterRowCount > beforeRowCount;
    Logger.log(`Direct sheet write: ${directWriteSuccess ? 'SUCCESS' : 'FAILED'}`);

    // Step 2: Test createDropdownList function
    Logger.log("Step 2: Testing createDropdownList function...");
    const testPayload = {
      name: 'Function Test List',
      description: 'Function test description',
      company_id: 'comp_function_test',
      options: [{label: 'Test Option', value: 'test_opt'}]
    };

    const beforeCount2 = listsSheet.getLastRow();
    const createdDropdown = createDropdownList(testPayload);
    const afterCount2 = listsSheet.getLastRow();

    Logger.log(`Function test - Before: ${beforeCount2}, After: ${afterCount2}`);
    Logger.log(`Created dropdown: ${JSON.stringify(createdDropdown)}`);
    const functionSuccess = afterCount2 > beforeCount2 && createdDropdown && createdDropdown.id;
    Logger.log(`createDropdownList function: ${functionSuccess ? 'SUCCESS' : 'FAILED'}`);

    // Step 3: Test doPost API layer
    Logger.log("Step 3: Testing doPost API layer...");
    const apiPayload = {
      action: 'createDropdownList',
      name: 'API Test List',
      description: 'API test description',
      company_id: 'comp_api_test',
      options: [{label: 'API Option', value: 'api_opt'}]
    };

    const beforeCount3 = listsSheet.getLastRow();
    const apiResponse = doPost({
      postData: { contents: JSON.stringify(apiPayload) }
    });
    const afterCount3 = listsSheet.getLastRow();

    Logger.log(`API test - Before: ${beforeCount3}, After: ${afterCount3}`);
    Logger.log(`API response: ${apiResponse.getContent()}`);
    const apiSuccess = afterCount3 > beforeCount3;
    Logger.log(`doPost API: ${apiSuccess ? 'SUCCESS' : 'FAILED'}`);

    // Step 4: Verify data retrieval
    Logger.log("Step 4: Testing data retrieval...");
    const allDropdowns = getDropdownLists();
    const directTestFound = allDropdowns.find(d => d.id === testId);
    const functionTestFound = allDropdowns.find(d => d.name === 'Function Test List');
    const apiTestFound = allDropdowns.find(d => d.name === 'API Test List');

    Logger.log(`Retrieval test - Direct: ${directTestFound ? 'FOUND' : 'NOT FOUND'}`);
    Logger.log(`Retrieval test - Function: ${functionTestFound ? 'FOUND' : 'NOT FOUND'}`);
    Logger.log(`Retrieval test - API: ${apiTestFound ? 'FOUND' : 'NOT FOUND'}`);

    // Step 5: Raw sheet data verification
    Logger.log("Step 5: Raw sheet data verification...");
    const allSheetData = listsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    Logger.log(`Total rows in sheet: ${allSheetData.length}`);
    Logger.log(`Sheet headers: ${JSON.stringify(allSheetData[0])}`);

    // Show last 3 rows to see our test data
    for (let i = Math.max(1, allSheetData.length - 3); i < allSheetData.length; i++) {
      Logger.log(`Row ${i + 1}: ${JSON.stringify(allSheetData[i])}`);
    }

    return {
      success: directWriteSuccess && functionSuccess && apiSuccess,
      results: {
        directWrite: directWriteSuccess,
        functionCall: functionSuccess,
        apiCall: apiSuccess,
        dataRetrieval: {
          direct: !!directTestFound,
          function: !!functionTestFound,
          api: !!apiTestFound
        },
        totalRows: allSheetData.length
      }
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in pipeline verification: ${error.message}`);
    Logger.log(`Error stack: ${error.stack}`);
    return {
      success: false,
      error: error.message
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Test the exact payload format that frontend sends
 * This simulates the actual frontend request to verify the fix
 */
function testFrontendDropdownCreation() {
  Logger.log("=== TESTING EXACT FRONTEND PAYLOAD FORMAT ===");

  try {
    // This is the exact payload format from your console logs
    const frontendPayload = {
      action: "createDropdownList",
      name: "Frontend Test List",
      description: "Test dropdown from frontend",
      company_id: "comp_1758704456776_ywbq16c39", // Test Company A
      options: [
        { value: "T1", label: "Value 1", parent_option_id: "", sort_order: 0 },
        { value: "T2", label: "Value 2", parent_option_id: "", sort_order: 1 }
      ]
    };

    Logger.log(`Frontend payload: ${JSON.stringify(frontendPayload)}`);

    // Test via doPost (exactly how frontend calls it)
    const response = doPost({
      postData: { contents: JSON.stringify(frontendPayload) }
    });

    const responseData = JSON.parse(response.getContent());
    Logger.log(`Response: ${JSON.stringify(responseData)}`);

    if (responseData.success && responseData.data) {
      const created = responseData.data;
      Logger.log(`✅ Created successfully with:`);
      Logger.log(`   ID: ${created.id}`);
      Logger.log(`   Name: ${created.name}`);
      Logger.log(`   Description: "${created.description}"`);
      Logger.log(`   Company ID: "${created.company_id}"`);

      // Verify it's saved with correct company filtering
      const companyDropdowns = getDropdownLists({ company_id: created.company_id });
      const found = companyDropdowns.find(d => d.id === created.id);

      Logger.log(`✅ Found in company filter: ${found ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`);

      if (found) {
        Logger.log(`   Found data: Name="${found.name}", Company="${found.company_id}"`);
        return {
          success: true,
          message: "Frontend payload test PASSED - dropdown created with correct company_id and found via filtering"
        };
      } else {
        return {
          success: false,
          message: "Created but not found in company filter"
        };
      }
    } else {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `Creation failed: ${JSON.stringify(responseData)}`
      };
    }

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error: ${error.message}`);
    return {
      success: false,
      error: error.message
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Comprehensive test of all filter scenarios that frontend will encounter
 * Tests all combinations: Global/Company creation with Global/Company filtering
 */
function testAllFilterScenarios() {
  Logger.log("=== COMPREHENSIVE FILTER SCENARIOS TEST ===");

  const testResults = {
    scenario1: null, // Global filter + Create global list
    scenario2: null, // Global filter + Create company-specific list
    scenario3: null, // Company filter + Create global list
    scenario4: null  // Company filter + Create company-specific list
  };

  try {
    const testCompanyId = "comp_1758704456776_ywbq16c39"; // Test Company A

    // SCENARIO 1: Create global list, verify with global filter
    Logger.log("\n--- SCENARIO 1: Create GLOBAL list, filter by GLOBAL ---");
    const globalPayload = {
      action: "createDropdownList",
      name: "Scenario1 Global List",
      description: "Global dropdown test",
      company_id: null, // Global
      options: [{ value: "G1", label: "Global Option 1" }]
    };

    const globalResponse = doPost({ postData: { contents: JSON.stringify(globalPayload) } });
    const globalData = JSON.parse(globalResponse.getContent());

    if (globalData.success) {
      const globalFiltered = getDropdownLists({ company_id: null });
      const foundInGlobal = globalFiltered.find(d => d.id === globalData.data.id);

      testResults.scenario1 = {
        created: !!globalData.data.id,
        company_id: globalData.data.company_id,
        foundInGlobalFilter: !!foundInGlobal,
        success: !!globalData.data.id && !!foundInGlobal
      };
      Logger.log(`Created: ${globalData.data.id}, Company: "${globalData.data.company_id}", Found: ${foundInGlobal ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`);
    }

    // SCENARIO 2: Create company list, test both filters
    Logger.log("\n--- SCENARIO 2: Create COMPANY list, test filters ---");
    const companyPayload = {
      action: "createDropdownList",
      name: "Scenario2 Company List",
      description: "Company dropdown test",
      company_id: testCompanyId,
      options: [{ value: "C1", label: "Company Option 1" }]
    };

    const companyResponse = doPost({ postData: { contents: JSON.stringify(companyPayload) } });
    const companyData = JSON.parse(companyResponse.getContent());

    if (companyData.success) {
      const globalFiltered2 = getDropdownLists({ company_id: null });
      const foundInGlobal2 = globalFiltered2.find(d => d.id === companyData.data.id);

      const companyFiltered2 = getDropdownLists({ company_id: testCompanyId });
      const foundInCompany2 = companyFiltered2.find(d => d.id === companyData.data.id);

      testResults.scenario2 = {
        created: !!companyData.data.id,
        company_id: companyData.data.company_id,
        foundInGlobalFilter: !!foundInGlobal2,
        foundInCompanyFilter: !!foundInCompany2,
        success: !!companyData.data.id && !foundInGlobal2 && !!foundInCompany2
      };
      Logger.log(`Created: ${companyData.data.id}, Company: "${companyData.data.company_id}"`);
      Logger.log(`Found in Global: ${foundInGlobal2 ? 'YES' : 'NO'}, Found in Company: ${foundInCompany2 ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`);
    }

    Logger.log("\n=== SCENARIO SUMMARY ===");
    Logger.log(`Scenario 1 (Global creation/filtering): ${testResults.scenario1?.success ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}`);
    Logger.log(`Scenario 2 (Company creation/filtering): ${testResults.scenario2?.success ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}`);

    const criticalPass = testResults.scenario1?.success && testResults.scenario2?.success;
    Logger.log(`\n🎯 CRITICAL SCENARIOS: ${criticalPass ? 'ALL PASS' : 'FAILED'}`);

    return {
      success: criticalPass,
      scenarios: testResults
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log(`Error in scenario testing: ${error.message}`);
    return {
      success: false,
      error: error.message,
      scenarios: testResults
    };
  }
}

      success: false,

      stack: error.stack
    };
  }

/**
 * Simple debugging function to check if dropdowns are actually being saved
 */
function checkDropdownPersistence() {
  console.log('🔍 PERSISTENCE CHECK: Examining dropdown_lists sheet directly');

  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const listsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.DROPDOWN_LISTS);

    if (!listsSheet) {
      console.error('❌ dropdown_lists sheet not found!');
      return { error: 'Sheet not found' };
    }

    const allData = listsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    console.log('📊 Total rows in sheet:', allData.length);

    if (allData.length <= 1) {
      console.log('⚠️ Only header row found, no dropdown data');
      return {
        success: true,
        totalRows: allData.length,
        headers: allData[0] || [],
        message: 'No dropdown data found'
      };
    }

    console.log('📋 Headers:', JSON.stringify(allData[0], null, 2));

    // Show recent entries
    const recentRows = allData.slice(-3);
    console.log('📈 Last 3 rows:');
    recentRows.forEach((row, index) => {
      console.log(`Row ${allData.length - 3 + index + 1}:`, JSON.stringify(row, null, 2));
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      totalRows: allData.length,
      hasData: allData.length > 1,
      headers: allData[0],
      lastEntries: recentRows
    };

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Error checking persistence:', error.message);
    return { error: error.message };
  }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * ENHANCED DROPDOWN CREATION DEBUGGING FUNCTIONS
 * Added to debug frontend-backend payload mismatch issues
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Logs the expected payload format for dropdown creation
 * This helps frontend developers understand the exact format needed
 */
function logExpectedDropdownPayload() {
  console.log('=== EXPECTED DROPDOWN CREATION PAYLOAD ===');

  const expectedPayload = {
    name: "Example Dropdown List",           // REQUIRED: string, dropdown list name
    description: "Example description",       // OPTIONAL: string, brief description
    company_id: null,                        // OPTIONAL: string or null (null = global)
    options: [                               // REQUIRED: array with at least one option
      {
        label: "High Priority",              // REQUIRED: string, display name
        value: "high",                       // REQUIRED: string, internal value
        parent_option_id: "",                // OPTIONAL: string, for hierarchical options
        sort_order: 0                        // OPTIONAL: number, display order
      },
      {
        label: "Medium Priority",
        value: "medium",
        parent_option_id: "",
        sort_order: 1
      },
      {
        label: "Low Priority",
        value: "low",
        parent_option_id: "",
        sort_order: 2
      }
    ]
  };

  console.log('Expected payload structure:');
  console.log(JSON.stringify(expectedPayload, null, 2));

  console.log('\n=== PAYLOAD VALIDATION RULES ===');
  console.log('1. name: REQUIRED, non-empty string');
  console.log('2. description: OPTIONAL, string (can be empty)');
  console.log('3. company_id: OPTIONAL, string or null (null = global access)');
  console.log('4. options: REQUIRED, array with at least one option');
  console.log('5. Each option must have: label (string), value (string)');
  console.log('6. Optional option fields: parent_option_id (string), sort_order (number)');

  return expectedPayload;
}

/**
 * Enhanced createDropdownList with comprehensive payload debugging
 */
function createDropdownListWithDebug(payload) {
  console.log('🚀 === DROPDOWN CREATION DEBUG SESSION START ===');
  console.log('📥 Raw payload received:', JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));

  try {
    // Log expected vs actual payload comparison
    const expectedFormat = logExpectedDropdownPayload();
    console.log('\n🔍 === PAYLOAD COMPARISON ===');

    // Check each required field
    const validationResults = {
      hasName: payload.hasOwnProperty('name') && typeof payload.name === 'string' && payload.name.trim().length > 0,
      hasOptions: payload.hasOwnProperty('options') && Array.isArray(payload.options) && payload.options.length > 0,
      hasValidDescription: !payload.hasOwnProperty('description') || typeof payload.description === 'string',
      hasValidCompanyId: !payload.hasOwnProperty('company_id') || payload.company_id === null || typeof payload.company_id === 'string'
    };

    console.log('✅ Validation Results:');
    Object.entries(validationResults).forEach(([key, result]) => {
      console.log(`   ${key}: ${result ? '✅ PASS' : '❌ FAIL'}`);
    });

    // Detailed option validation if options exist
    if (payload.options && Array.isArray(payload.options)) {
      console.log('\n🔍 Options Validation:');
      payload.options.forEach((option, index) => {
        const hasLabel = option.hasOwnProperty('label') && typeof option.label === 'string' && option.label.trim().length > 0;
        const hasValue = option.hasOwnProperty('value') && typeof option.value === 'string' && option.value.trim().length > 0;
        console.log(`   Option ${index + 1}: label=${hasLabel ? '✅' : '❌'}, value=${hasValue ? '✅' : '❌'}`);
      });
    }

    // Check if all validations passed
    const allValid = Object.values(validationResults).every(result => result);
    if (!allValid) {
      console.log('❌ Payload validation failed. Creation aborted.');
      throw new Error('Payload validation failed. Check console logs for details.');
    }

    console.log('✅ Payload validation passed. Proceeding with creation...');

    // Call the original createDropdownList function
    const result = createDropdownList(payload);

    console.log('🎉 Creation successful! Result:', JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
    console.log('🚀 === DROPDOWN CREATION DEBUG SESSION END ===\n');

    return result;

  } catch (error) {
    console.log('❌ Creation failed with error:', error.message);
    console.log('🚀 === DROPDOWN CREATION DEBUG SESSION END (WITH ERROR) ===\n');
    throw error;
  }
}

  console.log('🧪 === PAYLOAD COMPATIBILITY TEST ===');

/**
 * Update an existing custom field
 * @param {string} fieldId - Custom field ID
 * @param {Object} payload - Updated field data
 * @returns {Object} API response with updated field data
 */
function updateCustomField(fieldId, payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const customFieldsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);

    if (!customFieldsSheet) {
      throw new Error('Custom fields sheet not found');
    }

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload || Object.keys(payload).length === 0) {
      throw new Error('No update data provided');
    }

    const data = getSheetDataAsJSON(customFieldsSheet);
    const fieldIndex = data.findIndex(field => field.id === fieldId && field.is_active === true);

    if (fieldIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error(`Custom field not found: ${fieldId}`);
    }

    // Get current field data
    const currentField = data[fieldIndex];

    // Create update audit fields
    const updateFields = updateAuditFields();
    const sheetsUpdateFields = auditFieldsForSheets(updateFields);

    // Prepare updated row data
    const updatedRowData = [
      fieldId,                                           // id
      payload.ticket_type_id || currentField.ticket_type_id, // ticket_type_id
      payload.name || currentField.name,                 // name
      payload.label || currentField.label,               // label
      payload.type || currentField.type,                 // type
      payload.hasOwnProperty('is_required') ? payload.is_required : currentField.is_required, // is_required
      payload.hasOwnProperty('is_hidden') ? payload.is_hidden : currentField.is_hidden,       // is_hidden
      payload.sort_order || currentField.sort_order,     // sort_order
      payload.dropdown_list_id !== undefined ? payload.dropdown_list_id : currentField.dropdown_list_id, // dropdown_list_id
      payload.depends_on_field_id !== undefined ? payload.depends_on_field_id : currentField.depends_on_field_id, // depends_on_field_id
      sheetsUpdateFields.is_active,                       // is_active
      currentField.created_at,                            // created_at (preserve)
      currentField.created_by,                            // created_by (preserve)
      sheetsUpdateFields.updated_at,                      // updated_at
      sheetsUpdateFields.updated_by,                      // updated_by
      sheetsUpdateFields.deactivated_at,                  // deactivated_at
      sheetsUpdateFields.deactivated_by,                  // deactivated_by
      sheetsUpdateFields.deactivation_reason              // deactivation_reason
    ];

    // Update the row (fieldIndex + 2 for header row and 0-based index)
    const rowNumber = fieldIndex + 2;
    customFieldsSheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, updatedRowData.length).setValues([updatedRowData]);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction('UPDATE_CUSTOM_FIELD', fieldId, payload);

    Logger.log(`Updated custom field: ${fieldId}`);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        id: fieldId,
        ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id || currentField.ticket_type_id,
        name: payload.name || currentField.name,
        label: payload.label || currentField.label,
        type: payload.type || currentField.type,
        is_required: payload.hasOwnProperty('is_required') ? payload.is_required : currentField.is_required,
        is_hidden: payload.hasOwnProperty('is_hidden') ? payload.is_hidden : currentField.is_hidden,
        sort_order: payload.sort_order || currentField.sort_order,
        dropdown_list_id: payload.dropdown_list_id !== undefined ? payload.dropdown_list_id : currentField.dropdown_list_id,
        depends_on_field_id: payload.depends_on_field_id !== undefined ? payload.depends_on_field_id : currentField.depends_on_field_id,
        ...updateFields
      },
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateCustomField: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Soft delete a custom field
 * @param {string} fieldId - Custom field ID
 * @returns {Object} API response with deletion status
 */
function deleteCustomField(fieldId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const customFieldsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);

    if (!customFieldsSheet) {
      throw new Error('Custom fields sheet not found');
    }

    const data = getSheetDataAsJSON(customFieldsSheet);
    const fieldIndex = data.findIndex(field => field.id === fieldId && field.is_active === true);

    if (fieldIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error(`Custom field not found: ${fieldId}`);
    }

    // Create deactivation audit fields
    const deactivationFields = deleteAuditFields('Admin requested field deletion');
    const sheetsDeactivationFields = auditFieldsForSheets(deactivationFields);

    // Get current field data
    const currentField = data[fieldIndex];

    // Prepare deactivated row data
    const deactivatedRowData = [
      fieldId,                                            // id
      currentField.ticket_type_id,                       // ticket_type_id
      currentField.name,                                  // name
      currentField.label,                                 // label
      currentField.type,                                  // type
      currentField.is_required,                           // is_required
      currentField.is_hidden,                             // is_hidden
      currentField.sort_order,                            // sort_order
      currentField.dropdown_list_id,                      // dropdown_list_id
      currentField.depends_on_field_id,                   // depends_on_field_id
      sheetsDeactivationFields.is_active,                 // is_active (false)
      currentField.created_at,                            // created_at (preserve)
      currentField.created_by,                            // created_by (preserve)
      sheetsDeactivationFields.updated_at,                // updated_at
      sheetsDeactivationFields.updated_by,                // updated_by
      sheetsDeactivationFields.deactivated_at,            // deactivated_at
      sheetsDeactivationFields.deactivated_by,            // deactivated_by
      sheetsDeactivationFields.deactivation_reason        // deactivation_reason
    ];

    // Update the row (fieldIndex + 2 for header row and 0-based index)
    const rowNumber = fieldIndex + 2;
    customFieldsSheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, deactivatedRowData.length).setValues([deactivatedRowData]);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction('DELETE_CUSTOM_FIELD', fieldId, { reason: 'Admin requested field deletion' });

    Logger.log(`Deactivated custom field: ${fieldId}`);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        id: fieldId,
        deactivated: true,
        deactivated_at: deactivationFields.deactivated_at
      },
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteCustomField: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Reorder custom fields for a ticket type
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type identifier
 * @param {Array} fieldIds - Array of field IDs in desired order
 * @returns {Object} API response with reorder status
 */
function reorderCustomFields(ticketTypeId, fieldIds) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const customFieldsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);

    if (!customFieldsSheet) {
      throw new Error('Custom fields sheet not found');
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(fieldIds) || fieldIds.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('Field IDs array is required');
    }

    const data = getSheetDataAsJSON(customFieldsSheet);
    let updateCount = 0;

    // Update sort_order for each field
    fieldIds.forEach((fieldId, index) => {
      const fieldIndex = data.findIndex(field => field.id === fieldId && field.is_active === true && field.ticket_type_id === ticketTypeId);

      if (fieldIndex !== -1) {
        const newSortOrder = index + 1;
        const currentField = data[fieldIndex];

        // Create update audit fields
        const updateFields = updateAuditFields();
        const sheetsUpdateFields = auditFieldsForSheets(updateFields);

        // Prepare updated row data with new sort_order
        const updatedRowData = [
          fieldId,                                        // id
          currentField.ticket_type_id,                    // ticket_type_id
          currentField.name,                              // name
          currentField.label,                             // label
          currentField.type,                              // type
          currentField.is_required,                       // is_required
          currentField.is_hidden,                         // is_hidden
          newSortOrder,                                   // sort_order (updated)
          currentField.dropdown_list_id,                  // dropdown_list_id
          currentField.depends_on_field_id,               // depends_on_field_id
          sheetsUpdateFields.is_active,                   // is_active
          currentField.created_at,                        // created_at (preserve)
          currentField.created_by,                        // created_by (preserve)
          sheetsUpdateFields.updated_at,                  // updated_at
          sheetsUpdateFields.updated_by,                  // updated_by
          sheetsUpdateFields.deactivated_at,              // deactivated_at
          sheetsUpdateFields.deactivated_by,              // deactivated_by
          sheetsUpdateFields.deactivation_reason          // deactivation_reason
        ];

        // Update the row
        const rowNumber = fieldIndex + 2;
        customFieldsSheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, updatedRowData.length).setValues([updatedRowData]);
        updateCount++;
      }
    });

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction('REORDER_CUSTOM_FIELDS', ticketTypeId, { fieldIds, updateCount });

    Logger.log(`Reordered ${updateCount} custom fields for ticket type: ${ticketTypeId}`);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId,
        reordered_count: updateCount,
        field_ids: fieldIds
      },
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in reorderCustomFields: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Get custom field values for a ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket identifier
 * @returns {Object} API response with field values array
 */
function getCustomFieldValues(ticketId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const valuesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);

    if (!valuesSheet) {
      throw new Error('Custom field values sheet not found');
    }

    const data = getSheetDataAsJSON(valuesSheet);
    const fieldValues = data.filter(value => value.ticket_id === ticketId && value.is_active === true);

    Logger.log(`Retrieved ${fieldValues.length} custom field values for ticket: ${ticketId}`);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: fieldValues,
      count: fieldValues.length,
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getCustomFieldValues: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a new custom field value
 * @param {Object} payload - Field value data
 * @returns {Object} API response with created value data
 */
function createCustomFieldValue(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    let valuesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);

    if (!valuesSheet) {
      valuesSheet = initializeCustomFieldValuesSheet();
    }

    // Validate required fields
    if (!payload.ticket_id || !payload.custom_field_id) {
      throw new Error('Missing required fields: ticket_id, custom_field_id');
    }

    // Generate unique ID
    const valueId = 'cfv_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8);

    // Create audit fields
    const auditFields = createAuditFields();
    const sheetsAuditFields = auditFieldsForSheets(auditFields);

    // Prepare row data matching schema
    const rowData = [
      valueId,                                    // id
      payload.ticket_id,                          // ticket_id
      payload.custom_field_id,                    // custom_field_id
      payload.text_value || '',                   // text_value
      payload.number_value || null,               // number_value
      payload.date_value || '',                   // date_value
      payload.start_date_value || '',             // start_date_value (for date_range)
      payload.end_date_value || '',               // end_date_value (for date_range)
      payload.dropdown_option_id || '',           // dropdown_option_id
      sheetsAuditFields.is_active,                // is_active
      sheetsAuditFields.created_at,               // created_at
      sheetsAuditFields.created_by,               // created_by
      sheetsAuditFields.updated_at,               // updated_at
      sheetsAuditFields.updated_by,               // updated_by
      sheetsAuditFields.deleted_at,               // deleted_at
      sheetsAuditFields.deleted_by,               // deleted_by
      sheetsAuditFields.deletion_reason           // deletion_reason
    ];

    // Append row to sheet
    valuesSheet.appendRow(rowData);

    Logger.log(`Created custom field value: ${valueId}`);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        id: valueId,
        ticket_id: payload.ticket_id,
        custom_field_id: payload.custom_field_id,
        text_value: payload.text_value || '',
        number_value: payload.number_value || null,
        date_value: payload.date_value || '',
        start_date_value: payload.start_date_value || '',
        end_date_value: payload.end_date_value || '',
        dropdown_option_id: payload.dropdown_option_id || '',
        ...auditFields
      },
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createCustomFieldValue: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Update an existing custom field value
 * @param {string} valueId - Field value ID
 * @param {Object} payload - Updated value data
 * @returns {Object} API response with updated value data
 */
function updateCustomFieldValue(valueId, payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const valuesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);

    if (!valuesSheet) {
      throw new Error('Custom field values sheet not found');
    }

    // Validate payload exists
    if (!payload || Object.keys(payload).length === 0) {
      throw new Error('No update data provided');
    }

    const data = getSheetDataAsJSON(valuesSheet);
    const valueIndex = data.findIndex(value => value.id === valueId && value.is_active === true);

    if (valueIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error(`Custom field value not found: ${valueId}`);
    }

    // Get current value data
    const currentValue = data[valueIndex];

    // Create update audit fields
    const updateFields = updateAuditFields();
    const sheetsUpdateFields = auditFieldsForSheets(updateFields);

    // Prepare updated row data
    const updatedRowData = [
      valueId,                                                    // id
      currentValue.ticket_id,                                     // ticket_id (preserve)
      currentValue.custom_field_id,                               // custom_field_id (preserve)
      payload.text_value !== undefined ? payload.text_value : currentValue.text_value,           // text_value
      payload.number_value !== undefined ? payload.number_value : currentValue.number_value,     // number_value
      payload.date_value !== undefined ? payload.date_value : currentValue.date_value,           // date_value
      payload.start_date_value !== undefined ? payload.start_date_value : currentValue.start_date_value, // start_date_value
      payload.end_date_value !== undefined ? payload.end_date_value : currentValue.end_date_value,       // end_date_value
      payload.dropdown_option_id !== undefined ? payload.dropdown_option_id : currentValue.dropdown_option_id, // dropdown_option_id
      sheetsUpdateFields.is_active,                               // is_active
      currentValue.created_at,                                    // created_at (preserve)
      currentValue.created_by,                                    // created_by (preserve)
      sheetsUpdateFields.updated_at,                              // updated_at
      sheetsUpdateFields.updated_by,                              // updated_by
      sheetsUpdateFields.deleted_at,                              // deleted_at
      sheetsUpdateFields.deleted_by,                              // deleted_by
      sheetsUpdateFields.deletion_reason                          // deletion_reason
    ];

    // Update the row (valueIndex + 2 for header row and 0-based index)
    const rowNumber = valueIndex + 2;
    valuesSheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, updatedRowData.length).setValues([updatedRowData]);

    Logger.log(`Updated custom field value: ${valueId}`);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        id: valueId,
        ticket_id: currentValue.ticket_id,
        custom_field_id: currentValue.custom_field_id,
        text_value: payload.text_value !== undefined ? payload.text_value : currentValue.text_value,
        number_value: payload.number_value !== undefined ? payload.number_value : currentValue.number_value,
        date_value: payload.date_value !== undefined ? payload.date_value : currentValue.date_value,
        start_date_value: payload.start_date_value !== undefined ? payload.start_date_value : currentValue.start_date_value,
        end_date_value: payload.end_date_value !== undefined ? payload.end_date_value : currentValue.end_date_value,
        dropdown_option_id: payload.dropdown_option_id !== undefined ? payload.dropdown_option_id : currentValue.dropdown_option_id,
        ...updateFields
      },
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateCustomFieldValue: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Delete a custom field value
 * @param {string} valueId - Field value ID
 * @returns {Object} API response with deletion status
 */
function deleteCustomFieldValue(valueId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const valuesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);

    if (!valuesSheet) {
      throw new Error('Custom field values sheet not found');
    }

    const data = getSheetDataAsJSON(valuesSheet);
    const valueIndex = data.findIndex(value => value.id === valueId && value.is_active === true);

    if (valueIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error(`Custom field value not found: ${valueId}`);
    }

    // Create deletion audit fields
    const deletionFields = deleteAuditFields('User requested value deletion');
    const sheetsDeletionFields = auditFieldsForSheets(deletionFields);

    // Get current value data
    const currentValue = data[valueIndex];

    // Prepare deleted row data
    const deletedRowData = [
      valueId,                                            // id
      currentValue.ticket_id,                             // ticket_id
      currentValue.custom_field_id,                       // custom_field_id
      currentValue.text_value,                            // text_value
      currentValue.number_value,                          // number_value
      currentValue.date_value,                            // date_value
      currentValue.start_date_value,                      // start_date_value
      currentValue.end_date_value,                        // end_date_value
      currentValue.dropdown_option_id,                    // dropdown_option_id
      sheetsDeletionFields.is_active,                     // is_active (false)
      currentValue.created_at,                            // created_at (preserve)
      currentValue.created_by,                            // created_by (preserve)
      sheetsDeletionFields.updated_at,                    // updated_at
      sheetsDeletionFields.updated_by,                    // updated_by
      sheetsDeletionFields.deleted_at,                    // deleted_at
      sheetsDeletionFields.deleted_by,                    // deleted_by
      sheetsDeletionFields.deletion_reason                // deletion_reason
    ];

    // Update the row (valueIndex + 2 for header row and 0-based index)
    const rowNumber = valueIndex + 2;
    valuesSheet.getRange(rowNumber, 1, 1, deletedRowData.length).setValues([deletedRowData]);

    Logger.log(`Deleted custom field value: ${valueId}`);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        id: valueId,
        deleted: true,
        deleted_at: deletionFields.deleted_at
      },
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteCustomFieldValue: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate custom field values for a ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket identifier
 * @param {Array} values - Array of field values to validate
 * @returns {Object} API response with validation results
 */
function validateCustomFieldValues(ticketId, values) {
  try {
    if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
      throw new Error('Values must be an array');
    }

    const validationErrors = [];
    const validatedValues = [];

    // Get custom fields to validate against
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const customFieldsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);

    if (!customFieldsSheet) {
      throw new Error('Custom fields sheet not found');
    }

    const fieldsData = getSheetDataAsJSON(customFieldsSheet);
    const activeFields = fieldsData.filter(field => field.is_active === true);

    values.forEach((value, index) => {
      const field = activeFields.find(f => f.id === value.custom_field_id);

      if (!field) {
        validationErrors.push(`Unknown custom field: ${value.custom_field_id}`);
        return;
      }

      // Validate required fields
      if (field.is_required && !value.text_value && !value.number_value && !value.date_value && !value.dropdown_option_id && !value.start_date_value) {
        validationErrors.push(`Field '${field.name}' is required`);
        return;
      }

      // Validate field type constraints
      switch (field.type) {
        case 'number':
        case 'amount':
          if (value.number_value !== null && isNaN(value.number_value)) {
            validationErrors.push(`Field '${field.name}' must be a number`);
          }
          break;
        case 'date':
          if (value.date_value && !isValidDate(value.date_value)) {
            validationErrors.push(`Field '${field.name}' must be a valid date`);
          }
          break;
        case 'date_range':
          if (value.start_date_value && !isValidDate(value.start_date_value)) {
            validationErrors.push(`Field '${field.name}' start date must be valid`);
          }
          if (value.end_date_value && !isValidDate(value.end_date_value)) {
            validationErrors.push(`Field '${field.name}' end date must be valid`);
          }
          if (value.start_date_value && value.end_date_value && new Date(value.start_date_value) > new Date(value.end_date_value)) {
            validationErrors.push(`Field '${field.name}' start date must be before end date`);
          }
          break;
        case 'dropdown':
          if (value.dropdown_option_id && !field.dropdown_list_id) {
            validationErrors.push(`Field '${field.name}' has no dropdown list configured`);
          }
          break;
      }

      validatedValues.push({
        custom_field_id: value.custom_field_id,
        field_name: field.name,
        field_type: field.type,
        is_required: field.is_required,
        value: value,
        valid: true
      });
    });

    Logger.log(`Validated ${values.length} field values with ${validationErrors.length} errors`);

    return {
      status: validationErrors.length > 0 ? 'validation_errors' : 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        validated_values: validatedValues,
        validation_errors: validationErrors,
        total_values: values.length,
        error_count: validationErrors.length
      },
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateCustomFieldValues: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Bulk update custom field values for a ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket identifier
 * @param {Array} values - Array of field values to update
 * @returns {Object} API response with bulk update results
 */
function bulkUpdateCustomFieldValues(ticketId, values) {
  try {
    if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
      throw new Error('Values must be an array');
    }

    // First validate all values
    const validation = validateCustomFieldValues(ticketId, values);
    if (validation.status === 'validation_errors') {
      return validation;
    }

    const results = [];
    const errors = [];

    // Process each value
    values.forEach((value, index) => {
      try {
        let result;

        if (value.id) {
          // Update existing value
          result = updateCustomFieldValue(value.id, {
            text_value: value.text_value,
            number_value: value.number_value,
            date_value: value.date_value,
            start_date_value: value.start_date_value,
            end_date_value: value.end_date_value,
            dropdown_option_id: value.dropdown_option_id
          });
        } else {
          // Create new value
          result = createCustomFieldValue({
            ticket_id: ticketId,
            custom_field_id: value.custom_field_id,
            text_value: value.text_value,
            number_value: value.number_value,
            date_value: value.date_value,
            start_date_value: value.start_date_value,
            end_date_value: value.end_date_value,
            dropdown_option_id: value.dropdown_option_id
          });
        }

        if (result.status === 'success') {
          results.push(result.data);
        } else {
          errors.push({ index, error: result.error });
        }

      } catch (error) {
        errors.push({ index, error: error.toString() });
      }
    });

    Logger.log(`Bulk updated ${results.length} field values with ${errors.length} errors`);

    return {
      status: errors.length > 0 ? 'partial_success' : 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        updated_values: results,
        errors: errors,
        total_processed: values.length,
        success_count: results.length,
        error_count: errors.length
      },
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in bulkUpdateCustomFieldValues: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Search custom field values by criteria
 * @param {Object} criteria - Search criteria
 * @returns {Object} API response with search results
 */
function searchCustomFieldValues(criteria) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const valuesSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELD_VALUES);

    if (!valuesSheet) {
      throw new Error('Custom field values sheet not found');
    }

    const data = getSheetDataAsJSON(valuesSheet);
    let filteredValues = data.filter(value => value.is_active === true);

    // Apply filters based on criteria
    if (criteria.ticket_id) {
      filteredValues = filteredValues.filter(value => value.ticket_id === criteria.ticket_id);
    }

    if (criteria.custom_field_id) {
      filteredValues = filteredValues.filter(value => value.custom_field_id === criteria.custom_field_id);
    }

    if (criteria.text_value) {
      filteredValues = filteredValues.filter(value =>
        value.text_value && value.text_value.toLowerCase().includes(criteria.text_value.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    if (criteria.number_value !== undefined) {
      filteredValues = filteredValues.filter(value => value.number_value === criteria.number_value);
    }

    if (criteria.date_from) {
      filteredValues = filteredValues.filter(value =>
        value.date_value && new Date(value.date_value) >= new Date(criteria.date_from)
      );
    }

    if (criteria.date_to) {
      filteredValues = filteredValues.filter(value =>
        value.date_value && new Date(value.date_value) <= new Date(criteria.date_to)
      );
    }

    // Apply limit if specified
    if (criteria.limit && criteria.limit > 0) {
      filteredValues = filteredValues.slice(0, criteria.limit);
    }

    Logger.log(`Search returned ${filteredValues.length} custom field values`);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: filteredValues,
      count: filteredValues.length,
      criteria: criteria,
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in searchCustomFieldValues: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      error: error.toString(),
      timestamp: getCurrentTimestamp()
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Initialize Custom Fields Sheet
 * Creates the custom_fields table with proper schema
 */
function initializeCustomFieldsSheet() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);

  let customFieldsSheet;
  try {
    customFieldsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);
    if (customFieldsSheet) {
      Logger.log('Custom fields sheet already exists');
      return customFieldsSheet;
    }
  } catch (error) {
    // Sheet doesn't exist, create it
  }

  // Create the sheet
  customFieldsSheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.CUSTOM_FIELDS);

  // Set up headers matching DATABASE_SCHEMA_UPDATES.txt
  const headers = [
    'id',
    'ticket_type_id',
    'name',
    'label',
    'type',
    'is_required',
    'is_hidden',
    'sort_order',
    'dropdown_list_id',
    'depends_on_field_id',
    'is_active',
    'created_at',
    'created_by',
    'updated_at',
    'updated_by',
    'deactivated_at',
    'deactivated_by',
    'deactivation_reason'
  ];

  customFieldsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
  customFieldsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
  customFieldsSheet.setFrozenRows(1);

  Logger.log('Custom fields sheet initialized');
  return customFieldsSheet;
}

/**
 * Validate if a string is a valid date
 * @param {string} dateString - Date string to validate
 * @returns {boolean} True if valid date
 */
function isValidDate(dateString) {
  const date = new Date(dateString);
  return date instanceof Date && !isNaN(date);
}
