/**
 * Get workflow steps for a specific ticket type and company
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID
 * @param {string} companyId - Company ID (optional, null for global)
 * @returns {Object} Response with workflow steps data
 */
function getWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId = null, companyId = null) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    let steps = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const stepData = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        stepData[header] = row[index];
      });

      // Skip inactive/deleted steps
      if (stepData.is_active === false || stepData.deleted_at) {
        continue;
      }

      // Filter by ticket type and company
      if (ticketTypeId && stepData.ticket_type_id !== ticketTypeId) {
        continue;
      }

      if (companyId && stepData.company_id !== companyId) {
        continue;
      }

      steps.push(stepData);
    }

    // Sort by sort_order
    steps.sort((a, b) => (a.sort_order || 0) - (b.sort_order || 0));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: steps,
      message: `Retrieved ${steps.length} workflow steps`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a new workflow step
 * @param {Object} payload - Step data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created step data
 */
function createWorkflowStep(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['ticket_type_id', 'company_id', 'name', 'status_on_reach', 'step_type'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    // Generate unique ID
    const stepId = 'ws_' + Date.now();
    const now = new Date();

    // Get current user (mock for now)
    const currentUser = 'admin';

    // Set defaults and audit fields
    const stepData = {
      id: stepId,
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      name: payload.name,
      status_on_reach: payload.status_on_reach,
      step_type: payload.step_type || 'approval',
      approver_logic: payload.approver_logic || 'any',
      sort_order: payload.sort_order || 1,
      next_ticket_type_id: payload.next_ticket_type_id || null,
      external_app_url: payload.external_app_url || null,
      completion_action_name: payload.completion_action_name || null,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deactivated_at: null,
      deactivated_by: null,
      deactivation_reason: null
    };

    // Get headers and find the next row
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(stepData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CREATE_WORKFLOW_STEP', 'workflow_step', stepId, {
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      name: payload.name,
      step_type: payload.step_type
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: stepData,
      message: 'Workflow step created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update an existing workflow step
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID to update
 * @param {Object} payload - Updated step data
 * @returns {Object} Response with updated step data
 */
function updateWorkflowStep(stepId, payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the step
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === stepId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow step not found' };
    }

    // Get current user
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Update allowed fields
    const allowedFields = ['name', 'status_on_reach', 'step_type', 'approver_logic', 'sort_order',
                          'next_ticket_type_id', 'external_app_url', 'completion_action_name'];

    const changes = {};
    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (payload.hasOwnProperty(field)) {
        const colIndex = headers.indexOf(field);
        if (colIndex !== -1) {
          const oldValue = data[rowIndex][colIndex];
          sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, colIndex + 1).setValue(payload[field]);
          if (oldValue !== payload[field]) {
            changes[field] = { from: oldValue, to: payload[field] };
          }
        }
      }
    });

    // Update audit fields
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);

    // Log admin action
    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      logAdminAction(currentUser, 'UPDATE_WORKFLOW_STEP', 'workflow_step', stepId, changes);
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: stepId, changes: changes },
      message: 'Workflow step updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Delete (deactivate) a workflow step
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID to delete
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming deletion
 */
function deleteWorkflowStep(stepId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the step
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === stepId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow step not found' };
    }

    // Check if step is in use by active tickets
    // TODO: Add check for active tickets using this step

    // Soft delete by deactivating
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const deactivatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('deactivated_at');
    const deactivatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('deactivated_by');
    const deactivationReasonIndex = headers.indexOf('deactivation_reason');
    const isActiveIndex = headers.indexOf('is_active');

    if (deactivatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deactivatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (deactivatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deactivatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
    if (deactivationReasonIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deactivationReasonIndex + 1).setValue('Admin deletion');
    if (isActiveIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, isActiveIndex + 1).setValue(false);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'DELETE_WORKFLOW_STEP', 'workflow_step', stepId, {
      reason: 'Admin deletion',
      deactivated_at: now
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: stepId },
      message: 'Workflow step deactivated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Reorder workflow steps for a ticket type
 * @param {string} ticketTypeId - Ticket type ID
 * @param {Array} stepIds - Array of step IDs in desired order
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming reorder
 */
function reorderWorkflowSteps(ticketTypeId, stepIds) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(stepIds) || stepIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');
    const ticketTypeIndex = headers.indexOf('ticket_type_id');
    const sortOrderIndex = headers.indexOf('sort_order');
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (idIndex === -1 || ticketTypeIndex === -1 || sortOrderIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Required columns not found' };
    }

    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();
    let updatedCount = 0;

    // Update sort order for each step
    stepIds.forEach((stepId, index) => {
      const newSortOrder = index + 1;

      for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (data[i][idIndex] === stepId && data[i][ticketTypeIndex] === ticketTypeId) {
          sheet.getRange(i + 1, sortOrderIndex + 1).setValue(newSortOrder);
          if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(i + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
          if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(i + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
          updatedCount++;
          break;
        }
      }
    });

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'REORDER_WORKFLOW_STEPS', 'workflow_steps', ticketTypeId, {
      step_ids: stepIds,
      updated_count: updatedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { ticket_type_id: ticketTypeId, updated_count: updatedCount },
      message: `Reordered ${updatedCount} workflow steps`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in reorderWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Clone workflow steps from one ticket type to another
 * @param {string} fromTicketType - Source ticket type ID
 * @param {string} toTicketType - Target ticket type ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with cloned steps data
 */
function cloneWorkflowSteps(fromTicketType, toTicketType) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    // Get source steps
    const sourceSteps = getWorkflowSteps(fromTicketType);
    if (sourceSteps.status !== 'success' || !sourceSteps.data.length) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'No workflow steps found to clone' };
    }

    const clonedSteps = [];
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Clone each step
    for (const sourceStep of sourceSteps.data) {
      const newStepId = 'ws_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);

      const clonedStep = {
        id: newStepId,
        ticket_type_id: toTicketType,
        company_id: sourceStep.company_id,
        name: sourceStep.name,
        status_on_reach: sourceStep.status_on_reach,
        step_type: sourceStep.step_type,
        approver_logic: sourceStep.approver_logic,
        sort_order: sourceStep.sort_order,
        next_ticket_type_id: sourceStep.next_ticket_type_id,
        external_app_url: sourceStep.external_app_url,
        completion_action_name: sourceStep.completion_action_name,
        is_active: true,
        created_at: now,
        created_by: currentUser,
        updated_at: now,
        updated_by: currentUser,
        deactivated_at: null,
        deactivated_by: null,
        deactivation_reason: null
      };

      // Add to sheet
      const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
      const newRow = [];

      headers.forEach(header => {
        newRow.push(clonedStep[header] || '');
      });

      sheet.appendRow(newRow);
      clonedSteps.push(clonedStep);

      // Small delay to ensure unique timestamps
      Utilities.sleep(10);
    }

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CLONE_WORKFLOW_STEPS', 'workflow_steps', toTicketType, {
      from_ticket_type: fromTicketType,
      cloned_count: clonedSteps.length,
      step_ids: clonedSteps.map(step => step.id)
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: clonedSteps,
      message: `Cloned ${clonedSteps.length} workflow steps successfully`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in cloneWorkflowSteps: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get step approvers for a specific step
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with approver data
 */
function getStepApprovers(stepId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_approvers');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step approvers sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const approvers = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const approverData = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        approverData[header] = row[index];
      });

      if (approverData.step_id === stepId) {
        approvers.push(approverData);
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: approvers,
      message: `Retrieved ${approvers.length} step approvers`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Assign roles as approvers to a workflow step
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Array} roleIds - Array of role IDs to assign
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming assignments
 */
function assignStepApprovers(stepId, roleIds) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_approvers');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step approvers sheet not found' };
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(roleIds) || roleIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Role IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const currentUser = 'admin';
    let assignedCount = 0;

    // Check existing assignments to avoid duplicates
    const existingApprovers = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    const existingRoleIds = existingApprovers.data.map(approver => approver.role_id);

    roleIds.forEach(roleId => {
      if (!existingRoleIds.includes(roleId)) {
        sheet.appendRow([stepId, roleId]);
        assignedCount++;
      }
    });

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'ASSIGN_STEP_APPROVERS', 'step_approvers', stepId, {
      role_ids: roleIds,
      assigned_count: assignedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { step_id: stepId, assigned_count: assignedCount },
      message: `Assigned ${assignedCount} new approvers to step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in assignStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Remove role assignments from a workflow step
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @param {Array} roleIds - Array of role IDs to remove
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming removals
 */
function removeStepApprovers(stepId, roleIds) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_approvers');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step approvers sheet not found' };
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(roleIds) || roleIds.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Role IDs must be a non-empty array' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const stepIdIndex = headers.indexOf('step_id');
    const roleIdIndex = headers.indexOf('role_id');

    if (stepIdIndex === -1 || roleIdIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Required columns not found' };
    }

    let removedCount = 0;

    // Remove rows in reverse order to avoid index shifting
    for (let i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      const row = data[i];
      if (row[stepIdIndex] === stepId && roleIds.includes(row[roleIdIndex])) {
        sheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        removedCount++;
      }
    }

    // Log admin action
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'REMOVE_STEP_APPROVERS', 'step_approvers', stepId, {
      role_ids: roleIds,
      removed_count: removedCount
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { step_id: stepId, removed_count: removedCount },
      message: `Removed ${removedCount} approvers from step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in removeStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate step approver assignments
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID to validate
 * @returns {Object} Validation results
 */
function validateStepApprovers(stepId) {
  try {
    const approvers = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    if (approvers.status !== 'success') {
      return approvers;
    }

    const issues = [];

    if (approvers.data.length === 0) {
      issues.push('No approvers assigned to step');
    }

    // Check if all assigned roles exist and are active
    // TODO: Add role existence validation

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        step_id: stepId,
        approver_count: approvers.data.length,
        issues: issues,
        is_valid: issues.length === 0
      },
      message: issues.length === 0 ? 'Step approvers are valid' : `Found ${issues.length} validation issues`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateStepApprovers: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get user role assignments for a user and company
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @param {string} companyId - Company ID (optional)
 * @returns {Object} Response with user role assignments
 */
function getUserRoleAssignments(userId, companyId = null) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('user_role_assignments');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const assignments = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const assignmentData = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        assignmentData[header] = row[index];
      });

      // Skip inactive assignments
      if (assignmentData.is_active === false || assignmentData.deleted_at) {
        continue;
      }

      // Filter by user and company
      if (assignmentData.user_id === userId) {
        if (!companyId || assignmentData.company_id === companyId) {
          // Check validity end date
          if (assignmentData.validity_end_date &&
              new Date(assignmentData.validity_end_date) < new Date()) {
            continue; // Skip expired assignments
          }
          assignments.push(assignmentData);
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: assignments,
      message: `Retrieved ${assignments.length} role assignments`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getUserRoleAssignments: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a new user role assignment
 * @param {Object} payload - Assignment data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created assignment data
 */
function createUserRoleAssignment(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('user_role_assignments');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['user_id', 'role_id', 'company_id'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    // Check for duplicate assignment
    const existing = getUserRoleAssignments(payload.user_id, payload.company_id);
    if (existing.status === 'success') {
      const duplicate = existing.data.find(assignment =>
        assignment.role_id === payload.role_id &&
        assignment.ticket_type_id === payload.ticket_type_id
      );

      if (duplicate) {
        return { status: 'error', message: 'User already has this role assignment' };
      }
    }

    // Generate assignment ID
    const assignmentId = 'ura_' + Date.now();
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const assignmentData = {
      id: assignmentId,
      user_id: payload.user_id,
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id || null,
      role_id: payload.role_id,
      validity_end_date: payload.validity_end_date || null,
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deleted_at: null,
      deleted_by: null,
      deletion_reason: null
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(assignmentData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CREATE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignmentId, {
      user_id: payload.user_id,
      role_id: payload.role_id,
      company_id: payload.company_id
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: assignmentData,
      message: 'User role assignment created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update a user role assignment
 * @param {string} assignmentId - Assignment ID to update
 * @param {Object} payload - Updated assignment data
 * @returns {Object} Response with updated assignment data
 */
function updateUserRoleAssignment(assignmentId, payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('user_role_assignments');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the assignment
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === assignmentId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignment not found' };
    }

    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Update allowed fields
    const allowedFields = ['validity_end_date'];
    const changes = {};

    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (payload.hasOwnProperty(field)) {
        const colIndex = headers.indexOf(field);
        if (colIndex !== -1) {
          const oldValue = data[rowIndex][colIndex];
          sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, colIndex + 1).setValue(payload[field]);
          if (oldValue !== payload[field]) {
            changes[field] = { from: oldValue, to: payload[field] };
          }
        }
      }
    });

    // Update audit fields
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);

    // Log admin action
    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      logAdminAction(currentUser, 'UPDATE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignmentId, changes);
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: assignmentId, changes: changes },
      message: 'User role assignment updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Delete a user role assignment
 * @param {string} assignmentId - Assignment ID to delete
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming deletion
 */
function deleteUserRoleAssignment(assignmentId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('user_role_assignments');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the assignment
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === assignmentId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignment not found' };
    }

    // Soft delete
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const deletedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('deleted_at');
    const deletedByIndex = headers.indexOf('deleted_by');
    const deletionReasonIndex = headers.indexOf('deletion_reason');
    const isActiveIndex = headers.indexOf('is_active');

    if (deletedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (deletedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
    if (deletionReasonIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletionReasonIndex + 1).setValue('Admin deletion');
    if (isActiveIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, isActiveIndex + 1).setValue(false);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'DELETE_USER_ROLE_ASSIGNMENT', 'user_role_assignment', assignmentId, {
      reason: 'Admin deletion',
      deleted_at: now
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: assignmentId },
      message: 'User role assignment deleted successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get users who can approve a specific workflow step
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with user data
 */
function getUsersForStep(stepId) {
  try {
    // Get step approvers (roles)
    const stepApprovers = getStepApprovers(stepId);
    if (stepApprovers.status !== 'success') {
      return stepApprovers;
    }

    const roleIds = stepApprovers.data.map(approver => approver.role_id);
    if (roleIds.length === 0) {
      return {
        status: 'success',
        data: [],
        message: 'No roles assigned to this step'
      };
    }

    // Get all user role assignments for these roles
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const assignmentsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('user_role_assignments');

    if (!assignmentsSheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User role assignments sheet not found' };
    }

    const assignmentData = assignmentsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const assignmentHeaders = assignmentData[0];
    const eligibleUsers = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < assignmentData.length; i++) {
      const row = assignmentData[i];
      const assignment = {};

      assignmentHeaders.forEach((header, index) => {
        assignment[header] = row[index];
      });

      // Check if assignment is active and role matches
      if (assignment.is_active && roleIds.includes(assignment.role_id)) {
        // Check validity end date
        if (!assignment.validity_end_date ||
            new Date(assignment.validity_end_date) >= new Date()) {
          eligibleUsers.push({
            user_id: assignment.user_id,
            role_id: assignment.role_id,
            company_id: assignment.company_id,
            ticket_type_id: assignment.ticket_type_id,
            validity_end_date: assignment.validity_end_date
          });
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: eligibleUsers,
      message: `Found ${eligibleUsers.length} users eligible for this step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getUsersForStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate user role assignment data
 * @param {Object} payload - Assignment data to validate
 * @returns {Object} Validation results
 */
function validateUserRoleAssignment(payload) {
  try {
    const issues = [];

    // Check required fields
    const requiredFields = ['user_id', 'role_id', 'company_id'];
    requiredFields.forEach(field => {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        issues.push(`Missing required field: ${field}`);
      }
    });

    // Check validity end date format
    if (payload.validity_end_date) {
      if (!isValidDate(payload.validity_end_date)) {
        issues.push('Invalid validity_end_date format');
      } else if (new Date(payload.validity_end_date) <= new Date()) {
        issues.push('Validity end date must be in the future');
      }
    }

    // TODO: Add checks for user existence, role existence, company existence

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        is_valid: issues.length === 0,
        issues: issues
      },
      message: issues.length === 0 ? 'Assignment data is valid' : `Found ${issues.length} validation issues`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateUserRoleAssignment: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with SLA data
 */
function getStepSLAs(stepId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const slas = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const slaData = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        slaData[header] = row[index];
      });

      // Skip inactive SLAs
      if (slaData.is_active === false || slaData.deleted_at) {
        continue;
      }

      if (slaData.step_id === stepId) {
        slas.push(slaData);
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: slas,
      message: `Retrieved ${slas.length} SLA configurations`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getStepSLAs: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {Object} payload - SLA data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created SLA data
 */
function createStepSLA(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['step_id', 'duration', 'unit'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    // Validate unit
    const validUnits = ['hours', 'days'];
    if (!validUnits.includes(payload.unit)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Unit must be either "hours" or "days"' };
    }

    // Validate duration
    if (isNaN(payload.duration) || payload.duration <= 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Duration must be a positive number' };
    }

    // Check for existing SLA
    const existing = getStepSLAs(payload.step_id);
    if (existing.status === 'success' && existing.data.length > 0) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'SLA already exists for this step. Use update function instead.' };
    }

    const slaId = 'sla_' + Date.now();
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const slaData = {
      id: slaId,
      step_id: payload.step_id,
      duration: payload.duration,
      unit: payload.unit,
      exclude_weekends: payload.exclude_weekends || false,
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      deleted_at: null,
      deleted_by: null,
      deletion_reason: null
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(slaData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'CREATE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, {
      step_id: payload.step_id,
      duration: payload.duration,
      unit: payload.unit
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: slaData,
      message: 'Step SLA created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {string} slaId - SLA ID to update
 * @param {Object} payload - Updated SLA data
 * @returns {Object} Response with updated SLA data
 */
function updateStepSLA(slaId, payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the SLA
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === slaId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLA not found' };
    }

    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Validate fields if provided
    if (payload.unit && !['hours', 'days'].includes(payload.unit)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Unit must be either "hours" or "days"' };
    }

    if (payload.duration && (isNaN(payload.duration) || payload.duration <= 0)) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Duration must be a positive number' };
    }

    // Update allowed fields
    const allowedFields = ['duration', 'unit', 'exclude_weekends'];
    const changes = {};

    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (payload.hasOwnProperty(field)) {
        const colIndex = headers.indexOf(field);
        if (colIndex !== -1) {
          const oldValue = data[rowIndex][colIndex];
          sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, colIndex + 1).setValue(payload[field]);
          if (oldValue !== payload[field]) {
            changes[field] = { from: oldValue, to: payload[field] };
          }
        }
      }
    });

    // Update audit fields
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);

    // Log admin action
    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      logAdminAction(currentUser, 'UPDATE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, changes);
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: slaId, changes: changes },
      message: 'Step SLA updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Delete SLA configuration for a workflow step
 * @param {string} slaId - SLA ID to delete
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming deletion
 */
function deleteStepSLA(slaId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('step_slas');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLAs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the SLA
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === slaId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step SLA not found' };
    }

    // Soft delete
    const currentUser = 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    const deletedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('deleted_at');
    const deletedByIndex = headers.indexOf('deleted_by');
    const deletionReasonIndex = headers.indexOf('deletion_reason');
    const isActiveIndex = headers.indexOf('is_active');

    if (deletedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (deletedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);
    if (deletionReasonIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, deletionReasonIndex + 1).setValue('Admin deletion');
    if (isActiveIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, isActiveIndex + 1).setValue(false);

    // Log admin action
    logAdminAction(currentUser, 'DELETE_STEP_SLA', 'step_sla', slaId, {
      reason: 'Admin deletion',
      deleted_at: now
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: slaId },
      message: 'Step SLA deleted successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in deleteStepSLA: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Initialize workflow steps sheet with proper headers and audit fields
 * @returns {Object} Response indicating success or failure
 */
function initializeWorkflowStepsSheet() {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);

    let sheet;
    try {
      sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');
    } catch (e) {
      sheet = ss.insertSheet('workflow_steps');
    }

    // Clear existing content
    sheet.clear();

    // Define headers with comprehensive audit fields
    const headers = [
      'id',                    // Primary key
      'ticket_type_id',        // Foreign key to ticket_types
      'company_id',            // Foreign key to companies
      'name',                  // Step name
      'status_on_reach',       // Status when ticket reaches this step
      'step_type',             // 'approval', 'task', 'automated'
      'approver_logic',        // 'any', 'all'
      'sort_order',            // Step sequence
      'next_ticket_type_id',   // Chained workflow
      'external_app_url',      // External integration
      'completion_action_name', // Action name for completion
      'is_active',             // Active status
      'created_at',            // Creation timestamp
      'created_by',            // Creator user ID
      'updated_at',            // Last update timestamp
      'updated_by',            // Last updater user ID
      'deactivated_at',        // Deactivation timestamp
      'deactivated_by',        // Deactivator user ID
      'deactivation_reason'    // Reason for deactivation
    ];

    // Set headers
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);

    // Format headers
    const headerRange = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length);
    headerRange.setFontWeight('bold');
    headerRange.setBackground('#4285f4');
    headerRange.setFontColor('white');

    // Freeze header row
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);

    // Auto-resize columns
    sheet.autoResizeColumns(1, headers.length);

    Logger.log('Workflow steps sheet initialized successfully');

    return {
      status: 'success',
      message: 'Workflow steps sheet initialized with ' + headers.length + ' columns'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error initializing workflow steps sheet: ' + error.toString());
    return {
      status: 'error',
      message: 'Failed to initialize workflow steps sheet: ' + error.toString()
    };
  }
}
