/**
 * Create a new ticket and start its workflow
 * @param {Object} payload - Ticket creation data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created ticket data
 */
function createTicketWithWorkflow(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const ticketsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

    if (!ticketsSheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['title', 'ticket_type_id', 'requester_id', 'company_id'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    // Get ticket type and validate
    const ticketType = getTicketTypes(payload.ticket_type_id, payload.company_id);
    if (ticketType.status !== 'success' || !ticketType.data.length) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Invalid ticket type or company combination' };
    }

    // Generate ticket number
    const ticketNumber = generateTicketNumber(payload.ticket_type_id, payload.company_id);
    if (ticketNumber.status !== 'success') {
      return ticketNumber; // Return error from ticket number generation
    }

    // Get first workflow step
    const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(payload.ticket_type_id, payload.company_id);
    if (workflowSteps.status !== 'success' || !workflowSteps.data.length) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'No workflow steps defined for this ticket type' };
    }

    const firstStep = workflowSteps.data[0];

    // Generate unique ticket ID
    const ticketId = 'tk_' + Date.now();
    const now = new Date();
    const currentUser = payload.requester_id;

    // Calculate step due date
    const stepSLA = getStepSLAs(firstStep.id);
    let stepDueDate = null;
    if (stepSLA.status === 'success' && stepSLA.data.length > 0) {
      const sla = stepSLA.data[0];
      stepDueDate = calculateSLADueDate(now, sla.duration, sla.unit, sla.exclude_weekends);
    }

    // Create ticket data
    const ticketData = {
      id: ticketId,
      ticket_number: ticketNumber.data.ticket_number,
      title: payload.title,
      description: payload.description || '',
      ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
      requester_id: payload.requester_id,
      status: firstStep.status_on_reach,
      current_step_id: firstStep.id,
      step_due_date: stepDueDate,
      company_id: payload.company_id,
      priority: payload.priority || 'Medium',
      is_active: true,
      created_at: now,
      created_by: currentUser,
      updated_at: now,
      updated_by: currentUser,
      cancelled_at: null,
      cancelled_by: null,
      cancellation_reason: null
    };

    // Add to tickets sheet
    const headers = ticketsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, ticketsSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(ticketData[header] || '');
    });

    ticketsSheet.appendRow(newRow);

    // Create initial history entry
    const historyResult = createTicketHistoryEntry({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: currentUser,
      action: 'CREATED',
      comment: `Ticket created: ${payload.title}`,
      from_status: null,
      to_status: firstStep.status_on_reach,
      from_step_id: null,
      to_step_id: firstStep.id
    });

    // Create action log
    const actionLogResult = createTicketActionLog({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: currentUser,
      action_type: 'TICKET_CREATED',
      details: {
        ticket_number: ticketNumber.data.ticket_number,
        ticket_type_id: payload.ticket_type_id,
        initial_step: firstStep.id,
        initial_status: firstStep.status_on_reach
      }
    });

    // Process custom field values if provided
    if (payload.custom_field_values && Object.keys(payload.custom_field_values).length > 0) {
      const bulkUpdateResult = bulkUpdateCustomFieldValues(ticketId, payload.custom_field_values);
      if (bulkUpdateResult.status !== 'success') {
        Logger.log('Warning: Custom field values update failed: ' + bulkUpdateResult.message);
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: ticketData,
      message: 'Ticket created successfully and workflow started'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createTicketWithWorkflow: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Update ticket with validation and business rules
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID to update
 * @param {Object} payload - Updated ticket data
 * @returns {Object} Response with updated ticket data
 */
function updateTicketWithValidation(ticketId, payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
    }

    // Get current ticket data
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    // Check if ticket can be updated
    if (!ticket.is_active) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Cannot update inactive ticket' };
    }

    if (ticket.cancelled_at) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Cannot update cancelled ticket' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    if (idIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'ID column not found' };
    }

    // Find the ticket row
    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === ticketId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket not found' };
    }

    const currentUser = payload.updated_by || 'admin';
    const now = new Date();

    // Update allowed fields (restrict certain fields from being updated directly)
    const allowedFields = ['title', 'description', 'priority'];
    const changes = {};

    allowedFields.forEach(field => {
      if (payload.hasOwnProperty(field)) {
        const colIndex = headers.indexOf(field);
        if (colIndex !== -1) {
          const oldValue = data[rowIndex][colIndex];
          sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, colIndex + 1).setValue(payload[field]);
          if (oldValue !== payload[field]) {
            changes[field] = { from: oldValue, to: payload[field] };
          }
        }
      }
    });

    // Update audit fields
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(currentUser);

    // Create history entry if there were changes
    if (Object.keys(changes).length > 0) {
      const historyResult = createTicketHistoryEntry({
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        user_id: currentUser,
        action: 'UPDATED',
        comment: payload.comment || 'Ticket details updated',
        field_changes: changes
      });

      // Create action log
      createTicketActionLog({
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        user_id: currentUser,
        action_type: 'TICKET_UPDATED',
        details: changes
      });
    }

    // Update custom field values if provided
    if (payload.custom_field_values && Object.keys(payload.custom_field_values).length > 0) {
      const customFieldResult = bulkUpdateCustomFieldValues(ticketId, payload.custom_field_values);
      if (customFieldResult.status !== 'success') {
        Logger.log('Warning: Custom field update failed: ' + customFieldResult.message);
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: { id: ticketId, changes: changes },
      message: 'Ticket updated successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in updateTicketWithValidation: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Progress ticket through workflow steps
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} action - Action to perform (approve, reject, return, complete)
 * @param {Object} payload - Action data including comment, user_id
 * @returns {Object} Response with updated ticket state
 */
function progressTicketWorkflow(ticketId, action, payload) {
  try {
    // Get current ticket
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;
    const userId = payload.user_id;

    // Validate ticket can be progressed
    if (!ticket.is_active || ticket.cancelled_at) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Cannot progress inactive or cancelled ticket' };
    }

    // Check user permissions for current step
    const canApprove = checkUserCanApproveStep(userId, ticketId);
    if (canApprove.status !== 'success' || !canApprove.data.can_approve) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'User does not have permission to perform this action on current step' };
    }

    let nextStep = null;
    let newStatus = null;
    let actionType = '';

    switch (action.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'approve':
        // Calculate next workflow step
        const nextStepResult = calculateNextWorkflowStep(ticketId, 'APPROVE');
        if (nextStepResult.status !== 'success') {
          return nextStepResult;
        }

        nextStep = nextStepResult.data.next_step;
        newStatus = nextStep ? nextStep.status_on_reach : 'Completed';
        actionType = 'APPROVED';
        break;

      case 'reject':
        newStatus = 'Rejected';
        actionType = 'REJECTED';
        break;

      case 'return':
        // Find previous step
        const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticket.ticket_type_id, ticket.company_id);
        if (workflowSteps.status === 'success') {
          const currentStepIndex = workflowSteps.data.findIndex(step => step.id === ticket.current_step_id);
          if (currentStepIndex > 0) {
            nextStep = workflowSteps.data[currentStepIndex - 1];
            newStatus = nextStep.status_on_reach;
          } else {
            newStatus = 'Returned to Requester';
          }
        }
        actionType = 'RETURNED';
        break;

      case 'complete':
        newStatus = 'Completed';
        actionType = 'COMPLETED';
        break;

      default:
        return { status: 'error', message: 'Invalid action: ' + action };
    }

    // Update ticket
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === ticketId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket not found for update' };
    }

    const now = new Date();

    // Calculate new step due date
    let newStepDueDate = null;
    if (nextStep) {
      const stepSLA = getStepSLAs(nextStep.id);
      if (stepSLA.status === 'success' && stepSLA.data.length > 0) {
        const sla = stepSLA.data[0];
        newStepDueDate = calculateSLADueDate(now, sla.duration, sla.unit, sla.exclude_weekends);
      }
    }

    // Update ticket fields
    const statusIndex = headers.indexOf('status');
    const currentStepIndex = headers.indexOf('current_step_id');
    const stepDueDateIndex = headers.indexOf('step_due_date');
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (statusIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, statusIndex + 1).setValue(newStatus);
    if (currentStepIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, currentStepIndex + 1).setValue(nextStep ? nextStep.id : '');
    if (stepDueDateIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, stepDueDateIndex + 1).setValue(newStepDueDate || '');
    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(userId);

    // Create history entry
    const historyResult = createTicketHistoryEntry({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action: actionType,
      comment: payload.comment || `Ticket ${action}d`,
      from_status: ticket.status,
      to_status: newStatus,
      from_step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
      to_step_id: nextStep ? nextStep.id : null
    });

    // Create action log
    const actionLogResult = createTicketActionLog({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action_type: 'WORKFLOW_PROGRESSION',
      details: {
        action: action,
        from_status: ticket.status,
        to_status: newStatus,
        from_step: ticket.current_step_id,
        to_step: nextStep ? nextStep.id : null,
        comment: payload.comment
      }
    });

    // Handle step completion processing
    if (nextStep) {
      const completionResult = processStepCompletion(ticketId, ticket.current_step_id, action);
    }

    // Trigger notifications
    const notificationResult = triggerWorkflowNotifications(ticketId, nextStep ? nextStep.id : null, action);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        action_performed: action,
        new_status: newStatus,
        next_step: nextStep,
        step_due_date: newStepDueDate
      },
      message: `Ticket ${action}d successfully`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in progressTicketWorkflow: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Cancel a ticket with reason and audit trail
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID to cancel
 * @param {string} reason - Cancellation reason
 * @param {string} userId - User performing cancellation
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming cancellation
 */
function cancelTicketWithReason(ticketId, reason, userId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
    }

    // Get current ticket
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    // Validate cancellation
    if (!ticket.is_active) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket is already inactive' };
    }

    if (ticket.cancelled_at) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket is already cancelled' };
    }

    if (ticket.status === 'Completed') {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Cannot cancel completed ticket' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === ticketId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket not found' };
    }

    const now = new Date();

    // Update ticket with cancellation
    const statusIndex = headers.indexOf('status');
    const cancelledAtIndex = headers.indexOf('cancelled_at');
    const cancelledByIndex = headers.indexOf('cancelled_by');
    const cancellationReasonIndex = headers.indexOf('cancellation_reason');
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (statusIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, statusIndex + 1).setValue('Cancelled');
    if (cancelledAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, cancelledAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (cancelledByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, cancelledByIndex + 1).setValue(userId);
    if (cancellationReasonIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, cancellationReasonIndex + 1).setValue(reason);
    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(userId);

    // Create history entry
    const historyResult = createTicketHistoryEntry({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action: 'CANCELLED',
      comment: `Ticket cancelled: ${reason}`,
      from_status: ticket.status,
      to_status: 'Cancelled'
    });

    // Create action log
    const actionLogResult = createTicketActionLog({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action_type: 'TICKET_CANCELLED',
      details: {
        reason: reason,
        previous_status: ticket.status,
        previous_step: ticket.current_step_id
      }
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        cancelled_at: now,
        cancelled_by: userId,
        reason: reason
      },
      message: 'Ticket cancelled successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in cancelTicketWithReason: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Reopen a cancelled or rejected ticket to a specific step (admin function)
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID to reopen
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID to reopen to
 * @param {string} userId - Admin user performing reopen
 * @returns {Object} Response confirming reopen
 */
function reopenTicketToStep(ticketId, stepId, userId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
    }

    // Get current ticket
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    // Validate reopen conditions
    if (ticket.status !== 'Cancelled' && ticket.status !== 'Rejected') {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Can only reopen cancelled or rejected tickets' };
    }

    // Validate target step
    const targetStep = getWorkflowStepById(stepId);
    if (targetStep.status !== 'success') {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Invalid target step' };
    }

    const step = targetStep.data;

    // Verify step belongs to ticket's workflow
    if (step.ticket_type_id !== ticket.ticket_type_id || step.company_id !== ticket.company_id) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Step does not belong to ticket workflow' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const idIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

    let rowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][idIndex] === ticketId) {
        rowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (rowIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket not found' };
    }

    const now = new Date();

    // Calculate new step due date
    let newStepDueDate = null;
    const stepSLA = getStepSLAs(stepId);
    if (stepSLA.status === 'success' && stepSLA.data.length > 0) {
      const sla = stepSLA.data[0];
      newStepDueDate = calculateSLADueDate(now, sla.duration, sla.unit, sla.exclude_weekends);
    }

    // Update ticket to reopened state
    const statusIndex = headers.indexOf('status');
    const currentStepIndex = headers.indexOf('current_step_id');
    const stepDueDateIndex = headers.indexOf('step_due_date');
    const cancelledAtIndex = headers.indexOf('cancelled_at');
    const cancelledByIndex = headers.indexOf('cancelled_by');
    const cancellationReasonIndex = headers.indexOf('cancellation_reason');
    const updatedAtIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_at');
    const updatedByIndex = headers.indexOf('updated_by');

    if (statusIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, statusIndex + 1).setValue(step.status_on_reach);
    if (currentStepIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, currentStepIndex + 1).setValue(stepId);
    if (stepDueDateIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, stepDueDateIndex + 1).setValue(newStepDueDate || '');
    if (cancelledAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, cancelledAtIndex + 1).setValue('');
    if (cancelledByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, cancelledByIndex + 1).setValue('');
    if (cancellationReasonIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, cancellationReasonIndex + 1).setValue('');
    if (updatedAtIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedAtIndex + 1).setValue(now);
    if (updatedByIndex !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, updatedByIndex + 1).setValue(userId);

    // Create history entry
    const historyResult = createTicketHistoryEntry({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action: 'REOPENED',
      comment: `Ticket reopened to step: ${step.name}`,
      from_status: ticket.status,
      to_status: step.status_on_reach,
      from_step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
      to_step_id: stepId
    });

    // Create action log
    const actionLogResult = createTicketActionLog({
      ticket_id: ticketId,
      user_id: userId,
      action_type: 'TICKET_REOPENED',
      details: {
        reopened_from: ticket.status,
        reopened_to: step.status_on_reach,
        target_step: stepId,
        step_name: step.name
      }
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        ticket_id: ticketId,
        reopened_to_step: stepId,
        new_status: step.status_on_reach,
        step_due_date: newStepDueDate
      },
      message: 'Ticket reopened successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in reopenTicketToStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get complete ticket history/audit trail
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with ticket history data
 */
function getTicketHistory(ticketId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('ticket_history');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket history sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const history = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const historyEntry = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        historyEntry[header] = row[index];
      });

      if (historyEntry.ticket_id === ticketId) {
        history.push(historyEntry);
      }
    }

    // Sort by timestamp (most recent first)
    history.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp) - new Date(a.timestamp));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: history,
      message: `Retrieved ${history.length} history entries`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getTicketHistory: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a ticket history entry
 * @param {Object} payload - History entry data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created history entry
 */
function createTicketHistoryEntry(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('ticket_history');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket history sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['ticket_id', 'user_id', 'action'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    const historyId = 'th_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    const now = new Date();

    const historyData = {
      id: historyId,
      ticket_id: payload.ticket_id,
      user_id: payload.user_id,
      action: payload.action,
      comment: payload.comment || '',
      from_status: payload.from_status || null,
      to_status: payload.to_status || null,
      from_step_id: payload.from_step_id || null,
      to_step_id: payload.to_step_id || null,
      field_changes: payload.field_changes ? JSON.stringify(payload.field_changes) : null,
      timestamp: now
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(historyData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: historyData,
      message: 'History entry created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createTicketHistoryEntry: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get detailed system action logs for a ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with action logs data
 */
function getTicketActionLogs(ticketId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('ticket_action_logs');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket action logs sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const logs = [];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const logEntry = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        logEntry[header] = row[index];
      });

      if (logEntry.ticket_id === ticketId) {
        // Parse details JSON if it exists
        if (logEntry.details && typeof logEntry.details === 'string') {
          try {
            logEntry.details = JSON.parse(logEntry.details);
          } catch (e) {
            // Keep as string if not valid JSON
          }
        }
        logs.push(logEntry);
      }
    }

    // Sort by timestamp (most recent first)
    logs.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp) - new Date(a.timestamp));

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: logs,
      message: `Retrieved ${logs.length} action log entries`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getTicketActionLogs: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create a ticket action log entry
 * @param {Object} payload - Action log data
 * @returns {Object} Response with created log entry
 */
function createTicketActionLog(payload) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('ticket_action_logs');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket action logs sheet not found' };
    }

    // Validate required fields
    const requiredFields = ['ticket_id', 'user_id', 'action_type'];
    for (const field of requiredFields) {
      if (!payload[field]) {
        return { status: 'error', message: `Missing required field: ${field}` };
      }
    }

    const logId = 'tal_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    const now = new Date();

    const logData = {
      id: logId,
      ticket_id: payload.ticket_id,
      user_id: payload.user_id,
      action_type: payload.action_type,
      details: payload.details ? JSON.stringify(payload.details) : null,
      timestamp: now
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(logData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: logData,
      message: 'Action log entry created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createTicketActionLog: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Calculate the next workflow step based on current action
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} currentAction - Current action being performed
 * @returns {Object} Response with next step data
 */
function calculateNextWorkflowStep(ticketId, currentAction) {
  try {
    // Get current ticket
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    // Get all workflow steps for this ticket type
    const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticket.ticket_type_id, ticket.company_id);
    if (workflowSteps.status !== 'success') {
      return workflowSteps;
    }

    const steps = workflowSteps.data;
    const currentStepIndex = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === ticket.current_step_id);

    if (currentStepIndex === -1) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Current step not found in workflow' };
    }

    const currentStep = steps[currentStepIndex];
    let nextStep = null;

    switch (currentAction.toUpperCase()) {
      case 'APPROVE':
        // Check for conditional logic or chained workflows
        if (currentStep.next_ticket_type_id) {
          // This step creates a chained ticket - workflow continues to next step
          if (currentStepIndex + 1 < steps.length) {
            nextStep = steps[currentStepIndex + 1];
          }
          // Note: Chained ticket creation would be handled separately
        } else {
          // Normal progression to next step
          if (currentStepIndex + 1 < steps.length) {
            nextStep = steps[currentStepIndex + 1];
          }
          // If no next step, workflow is complete
        }
        break;

      case 'RETURN':
        // Return to previous step
        if (currentStepIndex > 0) {
          nextStep = steps[currentStepIndex - 1];
        }
        // If no previous step, return to requester
        break;

      case 'REJECT':
      case 'CANCEL':
        // No next step - workflow ends
        nextStep = null;
        break;

      default:
        return { status: 'error', message: 'Unknown action for workflow calculation' };
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        current_step: currentStep,
        next_step: nextStep,
        is_workflow_complete: nextStep === null && currentAction.toUpperCase() === 'APPROVE'
      },
      message: nextStep ? `Next step: ${nextStep.name}` : 'Workflow complete'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in calculateNextWorkflowStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Validate if a workflow transition is allowed
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} targetStepId - Target step ID
 * @returns {Object} Validation results
 */
function validateWorkflowTransition(ticketId, targetStepId) {
  try {
    // Get current ticket
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    // Get workflow steps
    const workflowSteps = getWorkflowSteps(ticket.ticket_type_id, ticket.company_id);
    if (workflowSteps.status !== 'success') {
      return workflowSteps;
    }

    const steps = workflowSteps.data;
    const currentStepIndex = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === ticket.current_step_id);
    const targetStepIndex = steps.findIndex(step => step.id === targetStepId);

    const issues = [];

    if (currentStepIndex === -1) {
      issues.push('Current step not found in workflow');
    }

    if (targetStepIndex === -1) {
      issues.push('Target step not found in workflow');
    }

    if (issues.length === 0) {
      // Check if transition is valid (can only go to adjacent steps or return to previous)
      const stepDifference = targetStepIndex - currentStepIndex;

      if (stepDifference === 1) {
        // Moving forward - always allowed
      } else if (stepDifference === -1) {
        // Moving backward (return) - allowed
      } else if (stepDifference === 0) {
        issues.push('Cannot transition to the same step');
      } else {
        issues.push('Can only transition to adjacent steps');
      }
    }

    // Check ticket state
    if (!ticket.is_active) {
      issues.push('Cannot transition inactive ticket');
    }

    if (ticket.cancelled_at) {
      issues.push('Cannot transition cancelled ticket');
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        is_valid: issues.length === 0,
        issues: issues,
        current_step_index: currentStepIndex,
        target_step_index: targetStepIndex
      },
      message: issues.length === 0 ? 'Workflow transition is valid' : `Found ${issues.length} validation issues`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in validateWorkflowTransition: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get approvers for current step of a ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with current step approvers
 */
function getApproversForCurrentStep(ticketId) {
  try {
    // Get current ticket
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    if (!ticket.current_step_id) {
      return {
        status: 'success',
        data: [],
        message: 'No current step - ticket may be completed or cancelled'
      };
    }

    // Get users who can approve this step
    const stepUsers = getUsersForStep(ticket.current_step_id);
    if (stepUsers.status !== 'success') {
      return stepUsers;
    }

    // Get step details for approver logic
    const stepDetails = getWorkflowStepById(ticket.current_step_id);
    let approverLogic = 'any';
    if (stepDetails.status === 'success') {
      approverLogic = stepDetails.data.approver_logic || 'any';
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        step_id: ticket.current_step_id,
        approver_logic: approverLogic,
        eligible_users: stepUsers.data,
        user_count: stepUsers.data.length
      },
      message: `Found ${stepUsers.data.length} eligible approvers for current step`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getApproversForCurrentStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Check if a user can approve a specific step of a ticket
 * @param {string} userId - User ID to check
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} Response indicating if user can approve
 */
function checkUserCanApproveStep(userId, ticketId) {
  try {
    // Get current step approvers
    const currentApprovers = getApproversForCurrentStep(ticketId);
    if (currentApprovers.status !== 'success') {
      return currentApprovers;
    }

    const eligibleUsers = currentApprovers.data.eligible_users;
    const canApprove = eligibleUsers.some(user => user.user_id === userId);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: {
        can_approve: canApprove,
        user_id: userId,
        step_id: currentApprovers.data.step_id,
        approver_logic: currentApprovers.data.approver_logic
      },
      message: canApprove ? 'User can approve this step' : 'User cannot approve this step'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in checkUserCanApproveStep: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Process step completion (handle chained workflows, external integrations)
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} stepId - Completed step ID
 * @param {string} action - Action that completed the step
 * @returns {Object} Response with processing results
 */
function processStepCompletion(ticketId, stepId, action) {
  try {
    // Get step details
    const stepDetails = getWorkflowStepById(stepId);
    if (stepDetails.status !== 'success') {
      return stepDetails;
    }

    const step = stepDetails.data;
    const results = [];

    // Handle chained workflow (create linked ticket)
    if (step.next_ticket_type_id && action.toLowerCase() === 'approve') {
      // Get current ticket details for chaining
      const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
      if (currentTicket.status === 'success') {
        const chainedTicketPayload = {
          title: `Chained from: ${currentTicket.data.title}`,
          ticket_type_id: step.next_ticket_type_id,
          requester_id: currentTicket.data.requester_id,
          company_id: currentTicket.data.company_id,
          parent_ticket_id: ticketId
        };

        const chainedResult = createTicketWithWorkflow(chainedTicketPayload);
        if (chainedResult.status === 'success') {
          // Create ticket link
          const linkResult = createTicketLink(ticketId, chainedResult.data.id);
          results.push({
            type: 'CHAINED_TICKET',
            success: true,
            data: chainedResult.data
          });
        } else {
          results.push({
            type: 'CHAINED_TICKET',
            success: false,
            error: chainedResult.message
          });
        }
      }
    }

    // Handle external app integration
    if (step.external_app_url) {
      // This would typically make an HTTP request to external system
      // For now, just log the integration point
      Logger.log(`External integration required: ${step.external_app_url}`);
      results.push({
        type: 'EXTERNAL_INTEGRATION',
        success: true,
        data: { url: step.external_app_url }
      });
    }

    // Handle completion actions
    if (step.completion_action_name) {
      Logger.log(`Completion action required: ${step.completion_action_name}`);
      results.push({
        type: 'COMPLETION_ACTION',
        success: true,
        data: { action: step.completion_action_name }
      });
    }

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: results,
      message: `Processed ${results.length} step completion actions`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in processStepCompletion: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Trigger workflow notifications for step changes
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {string} stepId - New step ID (null if workflow complete)
 * @param {string} action - Action that triggered the change
 * @returns {Object} Response with notification results
 */
function triggerWorkflowNotifications(ticketId, stepId, action) {
  try {
    const notifications = [];

    // Get ticket details
    const currentTicket = getTicketById(ticketId);
    if (currentTicket.status !== 'success') {
      return currentTicket;
    }

    const ticket = currentTicket.data;

    // Notify requester of status change
    notifications.push({
      type: 'STATUS_CHANGE',
      recipient: ticket.requester_id,
      message: `Ticket ${ticket.ticket_number}: ${action}d`,
      ticket_id: ticketId
    });

    // If there's a new step, notify the approvers
    if (stepId) {
      const stepApprovers = getUsersForStep(stepId);
      if (stepApprovers.status === 'success') {
        stepApprovers.data.forEach(approver => {
          notifications.push({
            type: 'APPROVAL_REQUEST',
            recipient: approver.user_id,
            message: `New approval required for ticket ${ticket.ticket_number}`,
            ticket_id: ticketId,
            step_id: stepId
          });
        });
      }
    }

    // Log notifications (in a real system, these would be sent via email/SMS/push)
    notifications.forEach(notification => {
      Logger.log(`NOTIFICATION: ${notification.type} to ${notification.recipient} - ${notification.message}`);
    });

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: notifications,
      message: `Triggered ${notifications.length} notifications`
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in triggerWorkflowNotifications: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get a single ticket by ID
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with ticket data
 */
function getTicketById(ticketId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('tickets');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Tickets sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const ticketData = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        ticketData[header] = row[index];
      });

      if (ticketData.id === ticketId) {
        return {
          status: 'success',
          data: ticketData,
          message: 'Ticket found'
        };
      }
    }

    return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket not found' };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getTicketById: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Get workflow step by ID
 * @param {string} stepId - Step ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with step data
 */
function getWorkflowStepById(stepId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('workflow_steps');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow steps sheet not found' };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      const stepData = {};

      headers.forEach((header, index) => {
        stepData[header] = row[index];
      });

      if (stepData.id === stepId) {
        return {
          status: 'success',
          data: stepData,
          message: 'Workflow step found'
        };
      }
    }

    return { status: 'error', message: 'Workflow step not found' };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in getWorkflowStepById: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Calculate SLA due date
 * @param {Date} startDate - Start date
 * @param {number} duration - Duration
 * @param {string} unit - 'hours' or 'days'
 * @param {boolean} excludeWeekends - Whether to exclude weekends
 * @returns {Date} Due date
 */
function calculateSLADueDate(startDate, duration, unit, excludeWeekends) {
  const dueDate = new Date(startDate);

  if (unit === 'hours') {
    dueDate.setHours(dueDate.getHours() + duration);
  } else if (unit === 'days') {
    let daysAdded = 0;
    while (daysAdded < duration) {
      dueDate.setDate(dueDate.getDate() + 1);

      // If excluding weekends, skip Saturday (6) and Sunday (0)
      if (!excludeWeekends || (dueDate.getDay() !== 0 && dueDate.getDay() !== 6)) {
        daysAdded++;
      }
    }
  }

  return dueDate;
}

/**
 * Create a ticket link (for chained workflows)
 * @param {string} parentTicketId - Parent ticket ID
 * @param {string} childTicketId - Child ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} Response with link data
 */
function createTicketLink(parentTicketId, childTicketId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('ticket_links');

    if (!sheet) {
      return { status: 'error', message: 'Ticket links sheet not found' };
    }

    const linkId = 'tl_' + Date.now();
    const linkData = {
      id: linkId,
      parent_ticket_id: parentTicketId,
      child_ticket_id: childTicketId,
      created_at: new Date()
    };

    // Add to sheet
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const newRow = [];

    headers.forEach(header => {
      newRow.push(linkData[header] || '');
    });

    sheet.appendRow(newRow);

    return {
      status: 'success',
      data: linkData,
      message: 'Ticket link created successfully'
    };

  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error in createTicketLink: ' + error.toString());
    return { status: 'error', message: error.toString() };
  }
}
