/**
 * =================================================================================
 * DATABASE SCHEMA ANALYZER FUNCTION - UPDATED September 27, 2025
 * Purpose: Analyze current Google Sheets database structure and document schema
 * Usage: Add this function to your APPSCRIPT.txt file and run analyzeCurrentSchema()
 * Output: Updates 'xSchema' sheet with complete database schema documentation
 *
 * UPDATES:
 * - Added Universal Entity Architecture tables (user_profile_types, role_types, user_profiles)
 * - Updated custom_fields and custom_field_values with entity_category column
 * - Enhanced user_role_assignments with date-based assignment features
 * - Added roles.role_type_id relationship
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Analyze current database schema and write to 'xSchema' sheet
 * This function examines all sheets in the spreadsheet and documents their structure
 */
function analyzeCurrentSchema() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);

  // Get the existing xSchema sheet and clear it
  let xSchemaSheet;
  try {
    xSchemaSheet = ss.getSheetByName('xSchema');
    xSchemaSheet.clear();
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error('xSchema sheet not found. Please create the xSchema sheet first.');
  }

  // Set up headers for schema documentation
  const headers = [
    'Sheet Name',
    'Column Count',
    'Column Headers',
    'Sample Row 2',
    'Row Count',
    'Status',
    'Notes'
  ];

  xSchemaSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setBackground('#4285f4');
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontColor('white');

  // Get all sheets in the spreadsheet
  const allSheets = ss.getSheets();
  const schemaData = [];

  // Define expected sheets from CLAUDE.md specification - UPDATED September 27, 2025
  // Added Universal Entity Architecture tables for user/role management
  const expectedSheets = {
    'companies': 'id|name|code|code_locked|code_locked_at|code_locked_reason|ticket_count + audit fields',
    'roles': 'id|role_type_id|name|company_id + audit fields',
    'tickets': 'id|ticket_number|title|ticket_type_id|requester_id|status|current_step_id|step_due_date|company_id + audit fields',
    'ticket_history': 'id|ticket_id|user_id|action|comment|timestamp',
    'ticket_types': 'id|transaction_id|code|name|description|is_active|require_attachment_on_create|company_id + audit fields',
    'comment_requirements': 'ticket_type_id|require_on_approve|require_on_return|require_on_reject|require_on_cancel + audit fields',
    'custom_fields': '🆕 id|ticket_type_id|entity_category|name|label|type|is_required|is_hidden|sort_order|dropdown_list_id|depends_on_field_id + audit fields',
    'custom_field_values': '🆕 id|ticket_id|entity_category|custom_field_id|text_value|number_value|date_value|start_date_value|end_date_value|dropdown_option_id + audit fields',
    'workflow_steps': 'id|ticket_type_id|company_id|name|status_on_reach|step_type|approver_logic|sort_order|next_ticket_type_id|external_app_url|completion_action_name + audit fields',
    'step_approvers': 'step_id|role_id + audit fields',
    'user_role_assignments': '🆕 id|user_id|role_id|ticket_type_id|company_id|assignment_type|effective_start_date|effective_end_date|auto_expire_days|assigned_by_user_id|approval_required|assignment_notes|validity_end_date + audit fields',
    'step_slas': 'step_id|duration|unit|exclude_weekends + audit fields',
    'step_conditions': 'id|step_id|custom_field_id|operator|value + audit fields',
    'dropdown_lists': 'id|name|description + audit fields',
    'dropdown_options': 'id|dropdown_list_id|label|value|parent_option_id|sort_order + audit fields',
    'dropdown_company_assignments': 'id|dropdown_list_id|company_id|is_global|is_active + audit fields',
    'ticket_attachments': 'id|ticket_id|uploader_id|file_name|file_url|uploaded_at + audit fields',
    'report_configurations': 'id|ticket_type_id|field_name|display_name|field_type|sort_order + audit fields',
    'ticket_links': 'id|parent_ticket_id|child_ticket_id|link_type + audit fields',
    'sequence_counters': 'id|sequence_name|last_number|company_id|ticket_type_code + audit fields',
    'ticket_action_logs': 'id|ticket_id|user_id|action_type|details|timestamp',
    'admin_action_logs': 'id|admin_user_id|action_type|target_entity|target_id|details|timestamp',
    'user_preferences': 'id|user_id|dark_mode|timezone|language|email_notifications|desktop_notifications|dashboard_layout|items_per_page|auto_refresh|refresh_interval + audit fields',
    // 🆕 NEW UNIVERSAL ENTITY ARCHITECTURE TABLES (September 27, 2025)
    'user_profile_types': '🆕 id|name|description|code|company_id + audit fields',
    'role_types': '🆕 id|name|description|code|company_id + audit fields',
    'user_profiles': '🆕 user_id|user_profile_type_id|display_name|email|immediate_approver_id|backup_approver_id|status|hire_date + audit fields'
  };

  // Analyze each sheet
  for (let i = 0; i < allSheets.length; i++) {
    const sheet = allSheets[i];
    const sheetName = sheet.getName();

    // Skip the xSchema sheet itself
    if (sheetName === 'xSchema') continue;

    try {
      const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
      const lastColumn = sheet.getLastColumn();

      let columnHeaders = '';
      let sampleRow = '';
      let status = '';
      let notes = '';

      if (lastRow >= 1 && lastColumn >= 1) {
        // Get headers (row 1)
        const headersRange = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastColumn);
        const headersValues = headersRange.getValues()[0];
        columnHeaders = headersValues.join('|');

        // Get sample data (row 2 if exists)
        if (lastRow >= 2) {
          const sampleRange = sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, lastColumn);
          const sampleValues = sampleRange.getValues()[0];
          sampleRow = sampleValues.map(val => val ? String(val).substring(0, 20) : '').join('|');
        } else {
          sampleRow = 'No data rows';
        }

        // Determine status and check for Universal Entity Architecture updates
        if (expectedSheets[sheetName]) {
          status = '✅ Expected';

          // Special checks for Universal Entity Architecture tables
          if (sheetName === 'custom_fields') {
            if (columnHeaders.includes('entity_category')) {
              notes = '🆕 Universal Entity Architecture: entity_category column added';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing entity_category column for Universal Entity support';
            }
          } else if (sheetName === 'custom_field_values') {
            if (columnHeaders.includes('entity_category')) {
              notes = '🆕 Universal Entity Architecture: entity_category column added';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing entity_category column for Universal Entity support';
            }
          } else if (sheetName === 'user_role_assignments') {
            if (columnHeaders.includes('assignment_type') && columnHeaders.includes('effective_start_date')) {
              notes = '🆕 Date-based role assignments enhanced';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing date-based assignment columns';
            }
          } else if (sheetName === 'roles') {
            if (columnHeaders.includes('role_type_id')) {
              notes = '🆕 Role types relationship added';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing role_type_id column';
            }
          } else if (['user_profile_types', 'role_types', 'user_profiles'].includes(sheetName)) {
            notes = '🆕 NEW: Universal Entity Architecture table';
          } else {
            // General audit field check
            if (columnHeaders.includes('is_active') && columnHeaders.includes('created_at')) {
              notes = 'Has audit fields';
            } else if (sheetName === 'ticket_history' || sheetName === 'ticket_action_logs' || sheetName === 'admin_action_logs') {
              notes = 'Immutable log table (no audit fields needed)';
            } else {
              notes = '⚠️ Missing audit fields';
            }
          }
        } else {
          status = '❓ Unexpected sheet';
          notes = 'Not in CLAUDE.md specification';
        }

      } else {
        columnHeaders = 'Empty sheet';
        sampleRow = 'No data';
        status = '❌ Empty';
        notes = 'Sheet exists but has no data';
      }

      schemaData.push([
        sheetName,
        lastColumn,
        columnHeaders,
        sampleRow,
        lastRow - 1, // Subtract 1 for header row
        status,
        notes
      ]);

    } catch (error) {
      schemaData.push([
        sheetName,
        0,
        'ERROR',
        'ERROR: ' + error.toString(),
        0,
        '❌ Error',
        'Failed to analyze sheet'
      ]);
    }
  }

  // Check for missing expected sheets
  for (const expectedSheet in expectedSheets) {
    const found = schemaData.some(row => row[0] === expectedSheet);
    if (!found) {
      schemaData.push([
        expectedSheet,
        0,
        expectedSheets[expectedSheet],
        'SHEET MISSING',
        0,
        '❌ Missing',
        'Required by CLAUDE.md specification'
      ]);
    }
  }

  // Sort data: existing sheets first, then missing sheets
  schemaData.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a[4] > 0 && b[4] === 0) return -1; // Existing sheets first
    if (a[4] === 0 && b[4] > 0) return 1;  // Missing sheets last
    return a[0].localeCompare(b[0]); // Alphabetical within groups
  });

  // Write data to sheet
  if (schemaData.length > 0) {
    xSchemaSheet.getRange(2, 1, schemaData.length, headers.length).setValues(schemaData);
  }

  // Add Universal Entity Architecture summary at the bottom
  const summaryStartRow = schemaData.length + 4;
  const existingSheets = schemaData.filter(row => row[4] > 0).length;
  const missingSheets = schemaData.filter(row => row[4] === 0).length;
  const sheetsWithAuditFields = schemaData.filter(row => row[6] === 'Has audit fields').length;
  const sheetsNeedingAuditFields = schemaData.filter(row => row[6] === '⚠️ Missing audit fields').length;
  const universalEntitySheets = schemaData.filter(row => row[6].includes('🆕')).length;

  const summary = [
    ['📊 SCHEMA ANALYSIS SUMMARY - UNIVERSAL ENTITY ARCHITECTURE'],
    [''],
    ['Total Sheets Found:', existingSheets],
    ['Missing Required Sheets:', missingSheets],
    ['Sheets with Audit Fields:', sheetsWithAuditFields],
    ['Sheets Needing Audit Fields:', sheetsNeedingAuditFields],
    ['Universal Entity Enhanced Sheets:', universalEntitySheets],
    [''],
    ['🎯 UNIVERSAL ENTITY ARCHITECTURE STATUS:'],
    ['✅ Custom Fields Infrastructure: Reusable for all entity types'],
    ['✅ User Profile Types: New table for configurable user profiles'],
    ['✅ Role Types: New table for configurable role types'],
    ['✅ Date-based Assignments: Enhanced role management'],
    [''],
    ['🔄 NEXT STEPS:'],
    ['1. Execute completeSystemReset() in Google Apps Script'],
    ['2. Create missing Universal Entity tables'],
    ['3. Add entity_category columns to custom_fields tables'],
    ['4. Enhance user_role_assignments with date-based features'],
    ['5. Test Universal Entity Architecture implementation']
  ];

  xSchemaSheet.getRange(summaryStartRow, 1, summary.length, 2).setValues(summary);
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(summaryStartRow, 1).setFontWeight('bold');
  xSchemaSheet.getRange(summaryStartRow, 1).setFontSize(12);

  // Format the sheet
  xSchemaSheet.autoResizeColumns(1, headers.length);
  xSchemaSheet.setFrozenRows(1);

  // Add conditional formatting for status column
  const statusRange = xSchemaSheet.getRange(2, 6, schemaData.length, 1);

  // Create rules for different statuses
  const rules = [];

  // Green for expected sheets
  rules.push(SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenTextContains('✅ Expected')
    .setBackground('#d4edda')
    .setRanges([statusRange])
    .build());

  // Red for missing/error sheets
  rules.push(SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenTextContains('❌')
    .setBackground('#f8d7da')
    .setRanges([statusRange])
    .build());

  // Yellow for unexpected sheets
  rules.push(SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenTextContains('❓')
    .setBackground('#fff3cd')
    .setRanges([statusRange])
    .build());

  xSchemaSheet.setConditionalFormatRules(rules);

  Logger.log('Universal Entity Architecture schema analysis complete. Results written to xSchema sheet.');

  return {
    status: 'success',
    message: 'Universal Entity Architecture schema analysis complete',
    totalSheets: existingSheets,
    missingSheets: missingSheets,
    sheetsWithAuditFields: sheetsWithAuditFields,
    sheetsNeedingAuditFields: sheetsNeedingAuditFields,
    universalEntitySheets: universalEntitySheets
  };
}

/**
 * Helper function to get detailed column analysis for a specific sheet
 * Enhanced for Universal Entity Architecture validation
 * @param {string} sheetName - Name of the sheet to analyze
 * @returns {Object} Detailed column analysis
 */
function analyzeSheetColumns(sheetName) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);

  try {
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
    const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    const lastColumn = sheet.getLastColumn();

    if (lastRow < 1 || lastColumn < 1) {
      return { error: 'Sheet is empty' };
    }

    const headersRange = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastColumn);
    const headers = headersRange.getValues()[0];

    const columnAnalysis = [];
    const universalEntityChecks = {
      hasEntityCategory: false,
      hasAuditFields: false,
      hasDateBasedAssignment: false,
      hasRoleTypeRelation: false
    };

    for (let col = 0; col < headers.length; col++) {
      const columnName = headers[col];
      let sampleValues = [];
      let dataTypes = new Set();

      // Check for Universal Entity Architecture columns
      if (columnName === 'entity_category') universalEntityChecks.hasEntityCategory = true;
      if (columnName === 'is_active' || columnName === 'created_at') universalEntityChecks.hasAuditFields = true;
      if (columnName === 'assignment_type' || columnName === 'effective_start_date') universalEntityChecks.hasDateBasedAssignment = true;
      if (columnName === 'role_type_id') universalEntityChecks.hasRoleTypeRelation = true;

      // Get sample values from first 5 data rows
      const maxSampleRows = Math.min(6, lastRow); // Row 1 is headers, so 2-6 for data
      if (maxSampleRows > 1) {
        const sampleRange = sheet.getRange(2, col + 1, maxSampleRows - 1, 1);
        const values = sampleRange.getValues();

        values.forEach(([value]) => {
          if (value !== null && value !== '') {
            sampleValues.push(String(value).substring(0, 30));
            dataTypes.add(typeof value);
          }
        });
      }

      columnAnalysis.push({
        position: col + 1,
        name: columnName,
        sampleValues: sampleValues.slice(0, 3), // Show first 3 samples
        dataTypes: Array.from(dataTypes),
        isEmpty: sampleValues.length === 0
      });
    }

    return {
      sheetName: sheetName,
      totalColumns: lastColumn,
      totalRows: lastRow - 1, // Exclude header
      columns: columnAnalysis,
      universalEntityStatus: universalEntityChecks
    };

  } catch (error) {
    return { error: error.toString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Create missing Universal Entity Architecture sheets based on CLAUDE.md specification
 * Creates the new tables required for user/role management
 */
function createUniversalEntitySheets() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);

  const universalEntitySheets = [
    {
      name: 'user_profile_types',
      headers: ['id', 'name', 'description', 'code', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason']
    },
    {
      name: 'role_types',
      headers: ['id', 'name', 'description', 'code', 'company_id', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'created_by', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason']
    },
    {
      name: 'user_profiles',
      headers: ['user_id', 'user_profile_type_id', 'display_name', 'email', 'immediate_approver_id', 'backup_approver_id', 'status', 'hire_date', 'is_active', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'updated_by', 'deactivated_at', 'deactivated_by', 'deactivation_reason']
    }
  ];

  const created = [];
  const skipped = [];

  universalEntitySheets.forEach(sheetConfig => {
    try {
      const existing = ss.getSheetByName(sheetConfig.name);
      skipped.push(sheetConfig.name + ' (already exists)');
    } catch (error) {
      // Sheet doesn't exist, create it
      const newSheet = ss.insertSheet(sheetConfig.name);

      // Add headers
      newSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheetConfig.headers.length).setValues([sheetConfig.headers]);
      newSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheetConfig.headers.length).setFontWeight('bold');
      newSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheetConfig.headers.length).setBackground('#4285f4');
      newSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheetConfig.headers.length).setFontColor('white');

      // Auto-resize columns
      newSheet.autoResizeColumns(1, sheetConfig.headers.length);

      created.push(sheetConfig.name);
    }
  });

  Logger.log('Universal Entity sheets created: ' + created.join(', '));
  Logger.log('Universal Entity sheets skipped: ' + skipped.join(', '));

  return {
    created: created,
    skipped: skipped,
    message: 'Universal Entity Architecture sheet creation complete. Run schema analyzer to verify structure.'
  };
}

/**
 * Validate Universal Entity Architecture implementation
 * Checks if all required columns and relationships are in place
 */
function validateUniversalEntityArchitecture() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);

  const validationResults = {
    status: 'success',
    checks: [],
    errors: [],
    warnings: []
  };

  // Check custom_fields has entity_category
  try {
    const customFieldsSheet = ss.getSheetByName('custom_fields');
    const headers = customFieldsSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, customFieldsSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];

    if (headers.includes('entity_category')) {
      validationResults.checks.push('✅ custom_fields has entity_category column');
    } else {
      validationResults.errors.push('❌ custom_fields missing entity_category column');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    validationResults.errors.push('❌ custom_fields sheet not found');
  }

  // Check custom_field_values has entity_category
  try {
    const customFieldValuesSheet = ss.getSheetByName('custom_field_values');
    const headers = customFieldValuesSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, customFieldValuesSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];

    if (headers.includes('entity_category')) {
      validationResults.checks.push('✅ custom_field_values has entity_category column');
    } else {
      validationResults.errors.push('❌ custom_field_values missing entity_category column');
    }
  } catch (error) {
    validationResults.errors.push('❌ custom_field_values sheet not found');
  }

  // Check for Universal Entity tables
  const requiredTables = ['user_profile_types', 'role_types', 'user_profiles'];
  requiredTables.forEach(tableName => {
    try {
      const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(tableName);
      validationResults.checks.push(`✅ ${tableName} table exists`);
    } catch (error) {
      validationResults.errors.push(`❌ ${tableName} table missing`);
    }
  });

  if (validationResults.errors.length > 0) {
    validationResults.status = 'failed';
  } else if (validationResults.warnings.length > 0) {
    validationResults.status = 'warning';
  }

  Logger.log('Universal Entity Architecture validation: ' + validationResults.status);

  return validationResults;
}

// Additional sheet definitions for tags and collaboration
const TAG_SHEETS = {
    TICKET_TAGS: "ticket_tags",
    TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS: "ticket_tag_assignments",
    TAG_CATEGORIES: "tag_categories",
    TAG_USAGE_STATISTICS: "tag_usage_statistics",
    TICKET_COLLABORATIONS: "ticket_collaborations",
    COLLABORATION_REQUESTS: "collaboration_requests",
    COLLABORATION_NOTIFICATIONS: "collaboration_notifications",
    SHARED_TICKET_ACCESS_LOGS: "shared_ticket_access_logs"
};

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * TICKET TAGS MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * Complete CRUD operations for ticket tagging system
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Get all ticket tags for a company (includes global tags)
 * @param {string} companyId - Company ID to filter tags
 * @param {boolean} includeGlobal - Whether to include global tags (default: true)
 * @returns {Object} API response with tags data
 */
function getTicketTags(companyId = null, includeGlobal = true) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const sheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);

        if (!sheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Ticket tags sheet not found");
        }

        const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const headers = data[0];
        const rows = data.slice(1);

        let tags = rows
            .filter(row => row[headers.indexOf('is_active')] === true)
            .map(row => ({
                id: row[headers.indexOf('id')],
                name: row[headers.indexOf('name')],
                color: row[headers.indexOf('color')],
                description: row[headers.indexOf('description')],
                tag_category: row[headers.indexOf('tag_category')],
                parent_tag_id: row[headers.indexOf('parent_tag_id')],
                company_id: row[headers.indexOf('company_id')],
                is_global: row[headers.indexOf('is_global')],
                usage_count: row[headers.indexOf('usage_count')] || 0,
                created_by_user_id: row[headers.indexOf('created_by_user_id')],
                created_at: row[headers.indexOf('created_at')],
                updated_at: row[headers.indexOf('updated_at')]
            }));

        // Filter by company and global settings
        if (companyId) {
            tags = tags.filter(tag =>
                tag.company_id === companyId ||
                (includeGlobal && tag.is_global === true)
            );
        }

        // Sort by usage count (most used first) then by name
        tags.sort((a, b) => {
            if (b.usage_count !== a.usage_count) {
                return b.usage_count - a.usage_count;
            }
            return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
        });

        return createSuccessResponse(tags);

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to get ticket tags: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * Create a new ticket tag
 * @param {Object} tagData - Tag information
 * @returns {Object} API response with created tag
 */
function createTicketTag(tagData) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const sheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);

        if (!sheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Ticket tags sheet not found");
        }

        // Generate unique tag ID
        const tagId = 'tag_' + generateId();
        const auditFields = createAuditFields(tagData.created_by_user_id);

        const newTag = {
            id: tagId,
            name: tagData.name,
            color: tagData.color || '#3B82F6',
            description: tagData.description || '',
            tag_category: tagData.tag_category || 'general',
            parent_tag_id: tagData.parent_tag_id || null,
            company_id: tagData.company_id || null,
            is_global: tagData.is_global || false,
            usage_count: 0,
            created_by_user_id: tagData.created_by_user_id,
            ...auditFields
        };

        // Add to sheet
        const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
        const newRow = headers.map(header => newTag[header] || '');
        sheet.appendRow(newRow);

        // Log admin action
        logAdminAction(tagData.created_by_user_id, 'CREATE_TAG', 'ticket_tag', tagId, {
            name: newTag.name,
            company_id: newTag.company_id,
            is_global: newTag.is_global
        });

        return createSuccessResponse(newTag);

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to create ticket tag: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * Update a ticket tag
 * @param {string} tagId - Tag ID to update
 * @param {Object} updateData - Updated tag information
 * @returns {Object} API response with updated tag
 */
function updateTicketTag(tagId, updateData) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const sheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);

        if (!sheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Ticket tags sheet not found");
        }

        const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const headers = data[0];
        const idColumnIndex = headers.indexOf('id');

        // Find the tag row
        const rowIndex = data.findIndex(row => row[idColumnIndex] === tagId);
        if (rowIndex === -1) {
            return createErrorResponse("Tag not found");
        }

        // Update the tag
        const updateFields = updateAuditFields(updateData.updated_by_user_id);
        const updatedTag = {};

        headers.forEach((header, index) => {
            if (updateData.hasOwnProperty(header)) {
                updatedTag[header] = updateData[header];
                sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, index + 1).setValue(updateData[header]);
            } else if (updateFields.hasOwnProperty(header)) {
                updatedTag[header] = updateFields[header];
                sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 1, index + 1).setValue(updateFields[header]);
            } else {
                updatedTag[header] = data[rowIndex][index];
            }
        });

        // Log admin action
        logAdminAction(updateData.updated_by_user_id, 'UPDATE_TAG', 'ticket_tag', tagId, updateData);

        return createSuccessResponse(updatedTag);

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to update ticket tag: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * Delete (deactivate) a ticket tag
 * @param {string} tagId - Tag ID to delete
 * @param {string} userId - User performing the deletion
 * @param {string} reason - Reason for deletion
 * @returns {Object} API response
 */
function deleteTicketTag(tagId, userId, reason = 'admin_request') {
    try {
        const deactivationFields = deactivateRecord(userId, reason);
        const updateResult = updateTicketTag(tagId, deactivationFields);

        if (updateResult.status === 'success') {
            // Log admin action
            logAdminAction(userId, 'DELETE_TAG', 'ticket_tag', tagId, { reason });
            return createSuccessResponse({ message: 'Tag deactivated successfully' });
        }

        return updateResult;

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to delete ticket tag: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * Assign tags to a ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {Array} tagIds - Array of tag IDs to assign
 * @param {string} assignedByUserId - User assigning the tags
 * @param {string} notes - Optional assignment notes
 * @returns {Object} API response
 */
function assignTagsToTicket(ticketId, tagIds, assignedByUserId, notes = '') {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const assignmentSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS);
        const tagSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);
        const ticketSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS);

        if (!assignmentSheet || !tagSheet || !ticketSheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Required sheets not found");
        }

        const assignments = [];

        // Create assignments for each tag
        for (const tagId of tagIds) {
            // Check if assignment already exists and is active
            const existingAssignment = findTagAssignment(ticketId, tagId);
            if (existingAssignment && existingAssignment.is_active) {
                continue; // Skip if already assigned
            }

            const assignmentId = 'tag_assign_' + generateId();
            const auditFields = createAuditFields(assignedByUserId);

            const assignment = {
                id: assignmentId,
                ticket_id: ticketId,
                tag_id: tagId,
                assigned_by_user_id: assignedByUserId,
                assignment_notes: notes,
                ...auditFields
            };

            // Add to assignments sheet
            const headers = assignmentSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, assignmentSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
            const newRow = headers.map(header => assignment[header] || '');
            assignmentSheet.appendRow(newRow);

            assignments.push(assignment);

            // Update tag usage count
            incrementTagUsage(tagId, assignedByUserId);
        }

        // Update ticket tag count
        updateTicketTagCount(ticketId);

        // Log action
        logTicketAction(ticketId, assignedByUserId, 'TAGS_ASSIGNED', {
            tag_ids: tagIds,
            assignment_notes: notes
        });

        return createSuccessResponse({
            assignments: assignments,
            message: `${assignments.length} tags assigned successfully`
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to assign tags: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * Remove tags from a ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @param {Array} tagIds - Array of tag IDs to remove
 * @param {string} removedByUserId - User removing the tags
 * @param {string} reason - Reason for removal
 * @returns {Object} API response
 */
function removeTagsFromTicket(ticketId, tagIds, removedByUserId, reason = 'user_request') {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const assignmentSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS);

        if (!assignmentSheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Tag assignments sheet not found");
        }

        const data = assignmentSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const headers = data[0];
        let removedCount = 0;

        // Find and deactivate assignments
        for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
            const row = data[i];
            const ticketIdCol = row[headers.indexOf('ticket_id')];
            const tagIdCol = row[headers.indexOf('tag_id')];
            const isActiveCol = row[headers.indexOf('is_active')];

            if (ticketIdCol === ticketId && tagIds.includes(tagIdCol) && isActiveCol === true) {
                // Deactivate this assignment
                const deletionFields = deleteRecord(removedByUserId, reason);
                headers.forEach((header, index) => {
                    if (deletionFields.hasOwnProperty(header)) {
                        assignmentSheet.getRange(i + 1, index + 1).setValue(deletionFields[header]);
                    }
                });
                removedCount++;
            }
        }

        // Update ticket tag count
        updateTicketTagCount(ticketId);

        // Log action
        logTicketAction(ticketId, removedByUserId, 'TAGS_REMOVED', {
            tag_ids: tagIds,
            removal_reason: reason
        });

        return createSuccessResponse({
            message: `${removedCount} tags removed successfully`
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to remove tags: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * Get tags assigned to a specific ticket
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID
 * @returns {Object} API response with assigned tags
 */
function getTicketAssignedTags(ticketId) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const assignmentSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS);
        const tagSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);

        if (!assignmentSheet || !tagSheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Required sheets not found");
        }

        // Get active assignments for this ticket
        const assignmentData = assignmentSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const assignmentHeaders = assignmentData[0];

        const activeAssignments = assignmentData.slice(1)
            .filter(row =>
                row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('ticket_id')] === ticketId &&
                row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('is_active')] === true
            )
            .map(row => ({
                id: row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('id')],
                tag_id: row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('tag_id')],
                assigned_by_user_id: row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('assigned_by_user_id')],
                assignment_notes: row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('assignment_notes')],
                created_at: row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('created_at')]
            }));

        // Get tag details
        const tagData = tagSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const tagHeaders = tagData[0];

        const tags = activeAssignments.map(assignment => {
            const tagRow = tagData.find(row =>
                row[tagHeaders.indexOf('id')] === assignment.tag_id &&
                row[tagHeaders.indexOf('is_active')] === true
            );

            if (tagRow) {
                return {
                    id: tagRow[tagHeaders.indexOf('id')],
                    name: tagRow[tagHeaders.indexOf('name')],
                    color: tagRow[tagHeaders.indexOf('color')],
                    description: tagRow[tagHeaders.indexOf('description')],
                    tag_category: tagRow[tagHeaders.indexOf('tag_category')],
                    assignment_notes: assignment.assignment_notes,
                    assigned_at: assignment.created_at,
                    assigned_by: assignment.assigned_by_user_id
                };
            }
            return null;
        }).filter(tag => tag !== null);

        return createSuccessResponse(tags);

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to get ticket tags: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * Search tickets by tags
 * @param {Array} tagIds - Array of tag IDs to search for
 * @param {string} operator - Search operator ('AND' or 'OR')
 * @param {string} companyId - Company ID filter (optional)
 * @returns {Object} API response with matching tickets
 */
function searchTicketsByTags(tagIds, operator = 'OR', companyId = null) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const assignmentSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS);
        const ticketSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS);

        if (!assignmentSheet || !ticketSheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Required sheets not found");
        }

        // Get active tag assignments
        const assignmentData = assignmentSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const assignmentHeaders = assignmentData[0];

        const activeAssignments = assignmentData.slice(1)
            .filter(row =>
                tagIds.includes(row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('tag_id')]) &&
                row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('is_active')] === true
            );

        // Group assignments by ticket
        const ticketTagMap = {};
        activeAssignments.forEach(row => {
            const ticketId = row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('ticket_id')];
            const tagId = row[assignmentHeaders.indexOf('tag_id')];

            if (!ticketTagMap[ticketId]) {
                ticketTagMap[ticketId] = [];
            }
            ticketTagMap[ticketId].push(tagId);
        });

        // Filter tickets based on operator
        const matchingTicketIds = Object.keys(ticketTagMap).filter(ticketId => {
            const ticketTags = ticketTagMap[ticketId];

            if (operator === 'AND') {
                return tagIds.every(tagId => ticketTags.includes(tagId));
            } else {
                return tagIds.some(tagId => ticketTags.includes(tagId));
            }
        });

        // Get ticket details
        const ticketData = ticketSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const ticketHeaders = ticketData[0];

        let tickets = ticketData.slice(1)
            .filter(row =>
                matchingTicketIds.includes(row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('id')]) &&
                row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('is_active')] === true &&
                (!companyId || row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('company_id')] === companyId)
            )
            .map(row => ({
                id: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('id')],
                ticket_number: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('ticket_number')],
                title: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('title')],
                ticket_type_id: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('ticket_type_id')],
                requester_id: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('requester_id')],
                status: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('status')],
                company_id: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('company_id')],
                created_at: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('created_at')],
                updated_at: row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('updated_at')],
                matched_tags: ticketTagMap[row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('id')]]
            }));

        return createSuccessResponse({
            tickets: tickets,
            search_criteria: {
                tag_ids: tagIds,
                operator: operator,
                company_id: companyId
            },
            total_matches: tickets.length
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to search tickets by tags: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * TICKET COLLABORATION MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
 * Complete collaboration and sharing system
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Share a ticket with user(s) or company
 * @param {string} ticketId - Ticket ID to share
 * @param {Object} shareData - Sharing configuration
 * @returns {Object} API response with collaboration details
 */
function shareTicket(ticketId, shareData) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const collaborationSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS);
        const ticketSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS);

        if (!collaborationSheet || !ticketSheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Required sheets not found");
        }

        // Validate ticket exists
        const ticketResult = getTicketById(ticketId);
        if (ticketResult.status !== 'success') {
            return createErrorResponse("Ticket not found");
        }

        const collaborationId = 'collab_' + generateId();
        const auditFields = createAuditFields(shareData.shared_by_user_id);

        const collaboration = {
            id: collaborationId,
            ticket_id: ticketId,
            shared_by_user_id: shareData.shared_by_user_id,
            shared_with_user_id: shareData.shared_with_user_id || null,
            shared_with_company_id: shareData.shared_with_company_id || null,
            permission_level: shareData.permission_level || 'viewer',
            can_view: shareData.can_view !== false,
            can_comment: shareData.can_comment || false,
            can_edit: shareData.can_edit || false,
            can_approve: shareData.can_approve || false,
            expiration_date: shareData.expiration_date || null,
            share_reason: shareData.share_reason || '',
            ...auditFields
        };

        // Add to collaborations sheet
        const headers = collaborationSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, collaborationSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
        const newRow = headers.map(header => collaboration[header] || '');
        collaborationSheet.appendRow(newRow);

        // Update ticket collaboration status
        updateTicketCollaborationStatus(ticketId);

        // Create notification
        createCollaborationNotification(collaborationId, 'TICKET_SHARED', shareData.shared_with_user_id,
            `Ticket ${ticketResult.data.ticket_number} has been shared with you`);

        // Log action
        logTicketAction(ticketId, shareData.shared_by_user_id, 'TICKET_SHARED', {
            shared_with_user_id: shareData.shared_with_user_id,
            shared_with_company_id: shareData.shared_with_company_id,
            permission_level: shareData.permission_level
        });

        return createSuccessResponse(collaboration);

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to share ticket: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * Get shared tickets for a user
 * @param {string} userId - User ID
 * @param {string} accessType - Type of access ('shared_by_me', 'shared_with_me', 'all')
 * @returns {Object} API response with shared tickets
 */
function getSharedTickets(userId, accessType = 'all') {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const collaborationSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_COLLABORATIONS);
        const ticketSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS);

        if (!collaborationSheet || !ticketSheet) {
            return createErrorResponse("Required sheets not found");
        }

        const collaborationData = collaborationSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const collaborationHeaders = collaborationData[0];

        // Filter collaborations based on access type
        let relevantCollaborations = collaborationData.slice(1)
            .filter(row => row[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('is_active')] === true);

        if (accessType === 'shared_by_me') {
            relevantCollaborations = relevantCollaborations.filter(row =>
                row[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('shared_by_user_id')] === userId
            );
        } else if (accessType === 'shared_with_me') {
            relevantCollaborations = relevantCollaborations.filter(row =>
                row[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('shared_with_user_id')] === userId
            );
        } else {
            relevantCollaborations = relevantCollaborations.filter(row =>
                row[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('shared_by_user_id')] === userId ||
                row[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('shared_with_user_id')] === userId
            );
        }

        // Get ticket details
        const ticketData = ticketSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const ticketHeaders = ticketData[0];

        const sharedTickets = relevantCollaborations.map(collaboration => {
            const ticketId = collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('ticket_id')];
            const ticketRow = ticketData.find(row =>
                row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('id')] === ticketId &&
                row[ticketHeaders.indexOf('is_active')] === true
            );

            if (ticketRow) {
                return {
                    collaboration_id: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('id')],
                    ticket_id: ticketId,
                    ticket_number: ticketRow[ticketHeaders.indexOf('ticket_number')],
                    title: ticketRow[ticketHeaders.indexOf('title')],
                    status: ticketRow[ticketHeaders.indexOf('status')],
                    shared_by_user_id: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('shared_by_user_id')],
                    shared_with_user_id: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('shared_with_user_id')],
                    permission_level: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('permission_level')],
                    can_view: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('can_view')],
                    can_comment: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('can_comment')],
                    can_edit: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('can_edit')],
                    can_approve: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('can_approve')],
                    expiration_date: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('expiration_date')],
                    shared_at: collaboration[collaborationHeaders.indexOf('created_at')]
                };
            }
            return null;
        }).filter(ticket => ticket !== null);

        return createSuccessResponse({
            shared_tickets: sharedTickets,
            access_type: accessType,
            total_count: sharedTickets.length
        });

    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("Failed to get shared tickets: " + error.message);
    }
}

/**
 * =================================================================================
 * UTILITY FUNCTIONS FOR TAGS AND COLLABORATION
 * Helper functions for tag and collaboration management
 * =================================================================================
 */

/**
 * Find tag assignment for ticket and tag
 */
function findTagAssignment(ticketId, tagId) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const sheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAG_ASSIGNMENTS);

        if (!sheet) return null;

        const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const headers = data[0];

        const assignmentRow = data.find(row =>
            row[headers.indexOf('ticket_id')] === ticketId &&
            row[headers.indexOf('tag_id')] === tagId &&
            row[headers.indexOf('is_active')] === true
        );

        if (assignmentRow) {
            const assignment = {};
            headers.forEach((header, index) => {
                assignment[header] = assignmentRow[index];
            });
            return assignment;
        }

        return null;
    } catch (error) {
        return null;
    }
}

/**
 * Increment tag usage count
 */
function incrementTagUsage(tagId, userId) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const tagSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.TICKET_TAGS);

        if (!tagSheet) return;

        // Update tag usage count
        const tagData = tagSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const tagHeaders = tagData[0];
        const idColumnIndex = tagHeaders.indexOf('id');
        const usageColumnIndex = tagHeaders.indexOf('usage_count');

        const tagRowIndex = tagData.findIndex(row => row[idColumnIndex] === tagId);
        if (tagRowIndex !== -1) {
            const currentCount = tagData[tagRowIndex][usageColumnIndex] || 0;
            tagSheet.getRange(tagRowIndex + 1, usageColumnIndex + 1).setValue(currentCount + 1);
        }

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error incrementing tag usage:', error);
    }
}

/**
 * Update ticket tag count
 */
function updateTicketTagCount(ticketId) {
    try {
        const activeTagsResult = getTicketAssignedTags(ticketId);
        if (activeTagsResult.status === 'success') {
            const tagCount = activeTagsResult.data.length;

            const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
            const ticketSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS);

            const ticketData = ticketSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
            const ticketHeaders = ticketData[0];
            const idColumnIndex = ticketHeaders.indexOf('id');
            const tagCountIndex = ticketHeaders.indexOf('tag_count');

            const ticketRowIndex = ticketData.findIndex(row => row[idColumnIndex] === ticketId);
            if (ticketRowIndex !== -1 && tagCountIndex !== -1) {
                ticketSheet.getRange(ticketRowIndex + 1, tagCountIndex + 1).setValue(tagCount);
            }
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error updating ticket tag count:', error);
    }
}

/**
 * Update ticket collaboration status
 */
function updateTicketCollaborationStatus(ticketId) {
    try {
        const sharedTicketsResult = getSharedTickets(null, 'all');
        if (sharedTicketsResult.status === 'success') {
            const ticketCollaborations = sharedTicketsResult.data.shared_tickets.filter(
                ticket => ticket.ticket_id === ticketId
            );

            const collaborationCount = ticketCollaborations.length;

            const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
            const ticketSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TICKETS);

            const ticketData = ticketSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
            const ticketHeaders = ticketData[0];
            const idColumnIndex = ticketHeaders.indexOf('id');

            const ticketRowIndex = ticketData.findIndex(row => row[idColumnIndex] === ticketId);
            if (ticketRowIndex !== -1) {
                const isCollaborativeIndex = ticketHeaders.indexOf('is_collaborative');
                const collaborationCountIndex = ticketHeaders.indexOf('collaboration_count');

                if (isCollaborativeIndex !== -1) {
                    ticketSheet.getRange(ticketRowIndex + 1, isCollaborativeIndex + 1).setValue(collaborationCount > 0);
                }
                if (collaborationCountIndex !== -1) {
                    ticketSheet.getRange(ticketRowIndex + 1, collaborationCountIndex + 1).setValue(collaborationCount);
                }
            }
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error updating ticket collaboration status:', error);
    }
}

/**
 * Create collaboration notification
 */
function createCollaborationNotification(collaborationId, notificationType, recipientUserId, message) {
    try {
        const spreadsheet = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SPREADSHEET_ID);
        const notificationSheet = spreadsheet.getSheetByName(TAG_SHEETS.COLLABORATION_NOTIFICATIONS);

        if (!notificationSheet) return;

        const notificationId = 'collab_notif_' + generateId();
        const notification = {
            id: notificationId,
            collaboration_id: collaborationId,
            notification_type: notificationType,
            recipient_user_id: recipientUserId,
            message: message,
            is_read: false,
            read_at: null,
            is_active: true,
            created_at: getCurrentTimestamp(),
            updated_at: getCurrentTimestamp()
        };

        const headers = notificationSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, notificationSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
        const newRow = headers.map(header => notification[header] || '');
        notificationSheet.appendRow(newRow);

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error creating collaboration notification:', error);
    }
}

/**
 * Main API endpoint handler for tags and collaboration
 * Extends the main doPost function with tag and collaboration endpoints
 */
function handleTagsCollaborationAPI(action, data) {
    try {
        switch (action) {
            // Tag management endpoints
            case 'getTags':
                return getTicketTags(data.companyId, data.includeGlobal);
            case 'createTag':
                return createTicketTag(data);
            case 'updateTag':
                return updateTicketTag(data.tagId, data.updateData);
            case 'deleteTag':
                return deleteTicketTag(data.tagId, data.userId, data.reason);
            case 'assignTags':
                return assignTagsToTicket(data.ticketId, data.tagIds, data.assignedByUserId, data.notes);
            case 'removeTags':
                return removeTagsFromTicket(data.ticketId, data.tagIds, data.removedByUserId, data.reason);
            case 'getTicketTags':
                return getTicketAssignedTags(data.ticketId);
            case 'searchTicketsByTags':
                return searchTicketsByTags(data.tagIds, data.operator, data.companyId);

            // Collaboration endpoints
            case 'shareTicket':
                return shareTicket(data.ticketId, data.shareData);
            case 'getSharedTickets':
                return getSharedTickets(data.userId, data.accessType);

            default:
                return createErrorResponse("Unknown action: " + action);
        }
    } catch (error) {
        return createErrorResponse("API Error: " + error.message);
    }
}
